> **▶ BUILD — The Soap Film — a new Workbench physics bench, a fresh geometry vein (minimal surfaces) (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=3`, fuel=7). I pulled the **Soap-Film / minimal-surface** exhibit seed — the freshest, flagged-lowest-risk pick, and a genuinely *new vein* the estate didn't have: minimal surfaces / calculus of variations in 2-D (the catenary's higher cousin). Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy), `soap-film/index.html` (~590 lines, ~150-line pure DOM-free CORE under a 3-D canvas render).
> - **The idea & why it's real physics, not a toy skin.** A soap film spanning two coaxial wire rings minimises its **area** (surface energy ∝ area). The *unique* minimal surface of revolution is the **catenoid** — `r(z) = a·cosh(z/a)`, literally "the catenary, spun about its axis." The neck `a` is fixed by the rings via the BVP `R = a·cosh(h/a)`. Two deep, falsifiable truths the bench makes visible: (1) a **minimal surface has mean curvature `H ≡ 0` everywhere** (the defining mark of a soap film — the two principal curvatures cancel); (2) the **Goldschmidt collapse** — pull the rings apart and the catenoid's area eventually exceeds *two flat discs* (crossover at `2h ≈ 1.0556·R` for equal rings), and past `2h ≈ 1.3255·R` the BVP has **no solution at all** (`R/h < cosh(u)/u`'s minimum `≈1.50888`) — so the physical film **snaps to two discs**. The exact same `cosh` curve as the workshop's catenary, one dimension up.
> - **The falsifiable spine is a pure DOM-free CORE** (`window.SoapFilmCore` / `module.exports`, so the headless self-test re-audits the *shipped* code): `solveCatenoidA(R,h)` (bisection on the BVP, null when no catenoid exists), `coshArea_a` (closed-form lateral area), `meanCurvatureCatenoid` (`H` from the two principal curvatures), `profileArea` (numeric surface-of-revolution integral), `discArea`/`cylinderArea` (competitors), `filmState` (film vs Goldschmidt-collapsed, with the *reason*: area-snap vs no-solution), and `relaxProfile` — a discrete **area-minimising relaxation** that lets a profile find its own minimum.
> - **Self-test 7/7**, every check exercising the real CORE: (1) **boundary** — solved neck gives `a·cosh(h/a)==R` across 7 ring/separation pairs (4.4e-16); (2) **minimal** — `max|H| == 0` everywhere on the catenoid (**2.22e-16**) — the soap-film condition, computed from the principal curvatures, not asserted; (3) **area** — closed-form `== ∫2πr√(1+r'²)dz` numeric integral (rel 8e-10); (4) **minimising** — catenoid area `<` cylinder AND `<` a cone-pair competitor; (5) **Goldschmidt** — the catenoid==two-disc crossover at `2h≈1.0556R` *and* no catenoid past `2h≈1.325R` (both scanned from the geometry — falsifiable: a wrong threshold would miss the crossover); (6) **relax** — area falls monotonically from a straight-cylinder seed (6.283→5.992) and converges to the catenoid profile (max dev **3.89e-5**); (7) **determinism**.
> - **The headless audit earned its keep — it caught a real bug.** I locked the CORE in a throwaway Node module (`/tmp/catenoid-core.mjs`) and ran the self-test headless **first**. The physics checks (BVP, `H≡0`, area, Goldschmidt at exactly the textbook 1.0556R) passed at machine precision immediately — but the **relaxation diverged** (area went *up*, profile dev 0.31): my first gradient descent used a fixed step that overshot wildly (`step·|grad| ≈ 0.7` per iter on a profile of radius ~1). I rewrote `relaxProfile` with a **backtracking line search** that guarantees a monotone area *decrease* every step (the physically honest statement: a soap film only ever lowers its surface energy) → 5→7 checks → converged to dev 0.0000. A watch-only demo would have shipped a "relaxation" that didn't relax.
> - **The picture & UX.** A rotating **3-D surface of revolution** on canvas (orthographic, painter-sorted translucent quads with a soft facing-shade, gold wire rings, highlight meridian ribs, a dashed symmetry axis). Drag the stage to rotate (auto-spin otherwise). The film renders **cyan** when it's a catenoid and **amber two flat discs joined by a vanishing thread** when collapsed — the snap is visually unmistakable. Panel: ring-radius + separation sliders, a **"Relax the film"** animation (morphs a straight cylinder down to the catenoid), a Show toggle (film/profile/both), a "vs. cylinder" overlay, PNG export, 6 frame presets (Fat neck → Classic waist → **The crossover 1.056R** → About to snap → Past the cliff → Two discs), and a live **facts** block (neck `a`, film area, two-disc area, max mean curvature, plus a state banner + an equation panel that explains *which* collapse path you're on).
> - **Browser-verified** (served origin :8761, agent-browser, unique session): chip reads "self-test 7/7 ✓"; canvas inks ~55k pixels; `ws:seen:soap-film` breadcrumb writes; at the classic waist the banner reads "Catenoid · minimal surface" with `max|H| = 1.1e-16`; the **Past-the-cliff preset (1.36R) flips the banner to "Snapped · two flat discs"** with neck "no solution" and the no-catenoid explanation, while **1.30R takes the *area-snap* Goldschmidt path** (catenoid exists but loses to the discs) — both branches confirmed live. Screenshots reviewed: the cyan catenoid and the amber disc-collapse both render in-aesthetic (dark/gold, serif title, mono panel).
> - **Integration:** added the Soap Film card (🫧) to the Workbench's *Toys & benches* group, placed right after **The Brachistochrone** — its natural sibling (both are calculus-of-variations / minimisation pieces; the catenary's higher cousin). No front-door POI (it's a Workbench bench), no forge artifact touched (`forge --check --all` still 29/29 current), no map change. Pruned the seed from ROADMAP (BLOOMED). `builds 3→4`, fuel 7→6 → the gauge now tips to **PLAN** next session (`builds ≥ 4`).

> **▶ BUILD — The Spirograph — a new Workbench bench, the curve-machines trio's third member (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=2`, fuel=8). I pulled the **Spirograph / hypotrochoid** exhibit seed — a fresh geometry vein, the lowest-risk of the freshest picks, and the one that *completes a family*: the gardener had noticed an emergent **"curve machines"** trio building (`epicycles/` = circles on circles · `harmonograph/` = swinging pendulums · the still-missing **gears in gears**). Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy), `spirograph/index.html` (~560 lines, ~150-line pure CORE under a DOM app).
> - **The idea & why it's real math, not a toy skin.** A pen pinned at offset `d` inside a small gear (radius `r`) rolling *without slipping* inside a fixed ring (radius `R`) traces a **hypotrochoid**; rolling *around the outside*, an **epitrochoid**. The childhood Spirograph's whole-number magic is provable: the curve **closes only after `R/gcd(R,r)` trips of the ring**, and that *same integer* is its **petal count** — so whole-number teeth give whole-number flowers (coprime gears → `R` petals; `gcd=g` → `R/g`). And a trochoid is **exactly a sum of two rotating vectors** — its Fourier-epicycle cousin with precisely two terms — the explicit tie back to `epicycles/`.
> - **The falsifiable spine is a pure DOM-free CORE** (`window.SpirographCore` / `module.exports`, so the headless self-test re-audits the *shipped* code): `gcd`, `closure(R,r)` (petals/trips/period from the gcd), `pen(R,r,d,t,inside)` (the parametric trochoid), `epicycleTerms`/`epicycleEval` (the two-vector decomposition), `rollFromSeed` (deterministic figures, petals kept in [4,60]).
> - **Self-test 7/7**, every check exercising the real CORE: (1) **closure** — `pen(period)==pen(0)` for 10 gear pairs × both modes (5.6e-13 px); (2) **minimal** — no earlier return inside `(0,period)`, so the closure period is the *true* period (falsifiable: an over-counted petal count would show an earlier return); (3) **petals** — counted radial maxima `== R/gcd(R,r)` (96/25→96, 60/36→5, 3/1→3 deltoid, 4/1→4 astroid, 80/21→80, 100/33→100); (4) **no-slip** — ring arc `R·t == r·φ_roll`, `φ_roll=(R/r)·t` (2.2e-16); (5) **two-vector** — `pen ==` sum of exactly two rotating vectors (**0.00e+0**); (6) **degenerate** — `d=0`→exact circle (`|pen|` const), `d=1`→cusps (deltoid min speed →3e-5≈0); (7) **determinism** — seed-pure, perturbation differs, petals in [4,60]. Plus independent Node cross-checks (`gcd` vs brute-force; deltoid=3/astroid=4; coprime 97/30→97).
> - **The headless audit earned its keep — it caught TWO real bugs, which I fixed in the *math*, not the test.** First run was **5/7**. (a) *no-slip*: I'd used the fixed-frame spin `(R∓r)/r·t` as the rolling angle; the correct rolling angle relative to the line of centres is `(R/r)·t` (the wheel makes `R/r` turns per lap) → corrected to 2.2e-16. (b) *two-vector*: `epicycleTerms` had the wrong sign **and** freq sign for the **outside** (epitrochoid) inner term — `pen()` outside is `−d·r·e^{+i·pe}`, so the term must be `amp:−d·r, freq:+(R+r)/r`, not `+d·r, −(R+r)/r`; fixed → match became **exact (0.00e+0)**. Both are the kind of subtle sign/frame error a watch-only demo would have shipped silently.
> - **The picture & UX.** A dark stage with the faint fixed ring; the rolling wheel drawn with its arm + a gold pen dot; the figure inked progressively in gold (glow). Panel: inside/outside segmented toggle, ring-teeth / wheel-teeth / pen-offset sliders, play/pause + restart + speed, hide-gears + PNG export, a **seed roll** field, 6 presets (Classic flower, Five-petal star, Dense rose, Spiked sun, Deltoid, Astroid), and a live **facts** block (`gcd`, `petals = R/gcd`, ring-trips-to-close, and the live parametric equation). View auto-fits any figure; trail is pre-sampled over exactly one period so it's mathematically closed.
> - **Browser-verified** (served origin :8743, agent-browser, unique session): chip reads "spirograph verified — 7/7 ✓"; **console clean** (0 error/warn lines); canvas inks pixels; `ws:seen:spirograph` breadcrumb writes; presets/seed-roll/mode-switch all drive the live facts + equation (Deltoid→3 petals, Spiked-sun→80 outside "epitrochoid", seed "workshop"→135/55 gcd 5 → 27 petals). Screenshots reviewed — figure + rolling gears render in-aesthetic (dark/gold, serif title, mono panel). *(Note: agent-browser's coordinate-click missed the small preset buttons twice; firing the buttons' real click handler confirmed the app logic is correct — a tooling hit-test quirk, not a bug.)*
> - **Integration:** added the Spirograph card (❋) to the Workbench's *Toys & benches* group, placed right after Fourier Epicycles to complete the curve-machines trio. No front-door POI (it's a Workbench toy), no forge artifact touched (`forge --check --all` still 29/29 current), no map change. Pruned the seed from ROADMAP (BLOOMED); updated the curve-machines observation row (the trio is now whole). `builds 2→3`, fuel 8→7 → still **BUILDs** next.

> **▶ BUILD — Cavern × Sound Garden: "Hear the Ladder" — a new 7th Quantum-drift bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=1`, fuel=9 after the last cross bloomed). I pulled the freshest, lowest-risk thread that *also* opens a genuinely untouched axis: the **Sound Garden had never met the Cavern** at all (only the Harmonograph, last session). The seed: *sonify a quantum energy ladder so you literally hear why the harmonic oscillator is harmonic.* Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree) as a new bench, `cavern/hear-the-ladder/index.html` (~430-line app over a ~120-line pure CORE).
> - **The idea (why it's real physics, not decoration).** The Box bench proves an energy ladder `Eₙ = n²π²/2` (rungs spreading as n²); the Oscillator bench proves `Eₙ = ω(n+½)` (rungs perfectly even). Map either ladder to a stack of audible tones (a fundamental f₀ and partials at f₀·ratio) and a deep truth becomes audible: the oscillator's *even* rungs are the **harmonic/overtone series** `1,2,3,4,…` — the partials of ONE periodic waveform — so they fuse into a single pure pitch (*this is literally why a spring/oscillator is "harmonic"*). The box's `n²` rungs give the **stretched** stack `1,4,9,16,…`; the partial spacing *widens*, so there is no single period — the ear hears a **struck bell**, a clang with no definite pitch. The same ladders the eye climbs next door, finally heard.
> - **The falsifiable spine is a pure, self-tested CORE** (DOM-free, Web-Audio-free, so the headless self-test re-audits it): `E_box`/`E_osc` reproduce the neighbouring benches' exact formulas; `boxPartialRatios`/`oscPartialRatios` give the partial stacks; `inharmonicity(ratios)` = the **relative spread of the gaps between consecutive partials** (the ear's actual cue: even spacing ⇒ one tone, stretching ⇒ bell) — it is **exactly 0** for the oscillator and **≈38%** for the box, **climbing as you stack more partials**; `voiceFreqs` scales the stack onto an audible fundamental and **drops (never aliases)** partials past a 7 kHz ceiling (the box, stretching, drops more); `partialAmps` is a 1/k roll-off and `masterGainFor` a no-clip master gain. I locked all of this in a throwaway Node module (`/tmp/htl/core.mjs`) *first* — and it caught a real subtlety: my first metric measured "distance to nearest integer", which the box's `n²=1,4,9,16` trivially passes (they're integers!). The correct, physically-honest signature is *spacing constancy*, so I rewrote the metric and the headline check.
> - **The Web Audio voicing.** Built lazily, **only inside a user gesture** (autoplay-safe): each partial is an oscillator (osc = `sine`, box = a slightly brighter `triangle`) → per-partial gain (1/k) → a master gain (no-clip) → destination, with short attack/release ramps. Two play buttons (🌀 Oscillator · pure, 📦 Box · bell) toggle a sustained stack; an **A–B** button plays the pure tone for ~1.7 s then the bell, so the contrast lands back-to-back in one breath. A partials slider (2–12) and a fundamental slider (60–220 Hz, named to the nearest note) re-voice live.
> - **The picture.** Two stacked panels (cool oscillator above, warm box below): each shows its **energy ladder** on the left (rungs at heights ∝ the ratio — the oscillator's evenly stepped, the box's climbing as n²) and its **voiced spectrum** on a shared **log-frequency axis** on the right, against a faint even "harmonic comb" so the eye can see the oscillator's partials land on the comb while the box's smear off it. Active (playing) partials glow; the rAF loop runs only while sound plays and parks itself otherwise.
> - **The estate-wide mute, honored.** Mute is the **shared `ws:pref:muted`** key — read on load, listened for via the `storage` event (muting in another estate page mutes here live), and a muted visitor pressing a play button **unmutes and plays** (the intuitive read of the gesture). Drops `ws:seen:physics-lab` on a direct visit (feeds the Survey of Heaven).
> - **Self-test 7/7**, every check calling the *shipped* CORE: (1) ladders reproduce the benches' formulas (box ratio n², osc even spacing/½ ground); (2) partials are the harmonic vs stretched series; (3) **the headline** — osc inharmonicity == 0 (0.00e+0), box ≫ 0 (38.1% at 8 partials); (4) box partial ≥ harmonic partial with the gap growing monotonically; (5) voicing keeps f₀ and is alias-safe (box drops more); (6) the 1/k stack can never clip (peak ≤ 0.9000). **Headless-Node** against the *actual embedded `runSelfTest()`* (extracted the inline CORE via a `Function`-construct, module-exports) → 7/7. **Live browser** (agent-browser, served origin :8742, unique session): badge `7/7 ✓ self-test`; readouts show osc `1·2·3·4·5·6` @ 0.0% and box `1·4·9·16·25·36` @ 34.3% (8-partial = 38.1%); clicking play toggles the button "on" and builds the graph; `ws:pref:muted` round-trips (a storage-event mute stops audio; a muted click unmutes→'0' and plays); the partials slider drives the box inharmonicity up to 42% at 12 partials; **zero console errors** across all of it (verified with an `error`/`console.error` hook); screenshots reviewed — the two-panel ladder+spectrum view is legible and in-aesthetic. Caught and fixed a float-dust display bug (`E_box(7)/E_box(1)` showed `48.9999…` → rounded the *display* only; the CORE math stays exact).
> - **Audio-Lens cross-check (the "verify what I can't hear" path).** Rendered the exact CORE's two stacks to WAV (`/tmp/htl/{osc,box}.wav`) and ran the `audio-lens` skill: the **oscillator** reads a clean **A2** with top peaks `A2·A3·E4` (110·220·330 Hz — a true overtone series) and a low **180 Hz spectral centroid** (warm, energy near the fundamental — fused); the **box** reads stretched peaks `A2·A4·B5` (110·440·990 = 1·,4·,9·f₀) and a high **442 Hz centroid** (bright, energy spread up — clangorous); **neither clips** (peaks 0.60 / 0.67). Spectrograms confirm by sight: the oscillator's partials are a tight even comb low in the frame; the box's spread apart and climb. The physics, the metric, the numbers, AND the picture all agree.
> - **Integration:** added the bench card (🔔) to the Quantum-drift list on `cavern/index.html`; the Cavern is now a **7-bench** Quantum drift (10 benches overall). Pruned the seed from ROADMAP (BLOOMED) and bumped its metagame row + the GRAND room seed. `builds 1→2`, fuel 9→8 → still **BUILDs** next.

> **▶ BUILD — Harmonograph × Sound Garden: "Hear the figure" — the bloomed cross (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=0`, fuel=10 after the planting pass below). I pulled the seed the gardener had just flagged **NOW RIPE** three times over — `Harmonograph × Sound Garden` — because it was the freshest, lowest-risk thread *and* it opens a genuinely untouched axis: the **Sound Garden had never met the Harmonograph** (only its visual cousin, Epicycles). The visual half already shipped (`harmonograph/index.html`, 5/5), so the build was a focused enrichment of one existing file, not a new room. Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree); `harmonograph/index.html` grew ~150 lines.
> - **The idea (why it's a real cross, not decoration).** A harmonograph figure *is* a Lissajous curve, and a Lissajous curve's whole identity is its **frequency ratio** fx:fy. That ratio is also a **musical interval**: 2:3 is a perfect fifth, 1:2 an octave, 4:5 a major third. So the same number that draws the petals can be *heard* — consonant figures sound consonant. And the tiny **detune** that makes the petals visibly "breathe" (the precession the v1 lede already advertises) is, sonically, exactly a **beat** between two near-equal tones. The sight and the sound are the *same physics*; the cross just exposes the ear to what the eye already sees.
> - **The mapping is a pure, self-tested CORE** (DOM-free, Web-Audio-free, so the headless self-test re-audits it): `intervalFromRatio(fx,fy)` reduces the live config's fundamental fy/fx to the small-integer interval p:q; `voiceFreqs(fx,fy,base)` returns the two voice frequencies — x at A3 (220 Hz), y at `base · (raw fy/fx)` **folded into ~[1,2]** so the interval stays in one comfortable octave **but keeps the detune** (so beats survive the fold); `masterGainFor(n,amp,ceiling)` is a headroom-safe master gain that can never let the summed peak clip. I locked all of this in a throwaway Node harness (`/tmp/hg-audio-verify.mjs`, 5/5) *before* touching the page, then ported it verbatim into the CORE.
> - **The Web Audio voicing.** Built lazily, **only inside a user gesture** (autoplay-safe): two oscillators — x a warm `triangle`, y a pure `sine` — → per-voice gain → master gain → a gentle `DynamicsCompressor` → destination. The master fades up/down smoothly (`linearRampToValueAtTime`), and switching a preset/seed **glides** the y-voice to the new pitch (`setTargetAtTime`) so the interval audibly *slurs* between figures. `updateRatioTag()` — the single chokepoint every config change already calls — now also calls `retuneAudio()`, so the ear can never drift from the eye.
> - **The estate-wide mute, honored properly.** A new **♪ Listen** button in the Motion row, with an interval readout beside it that names what you hear ("2:3 · perfect fifth"). Mute is the **shared `ws:pref:muted`** key — read on load, written on toggle, and *listened for* via the `storage` event (so muting in another estate page mutes here live). A muted visitor pressing Listen unmutes **and** plays (the intuitive read of the gesture). Silent until the gesture; respects the autoplay gate.
> - **Self-test 5/5 → 9/9**, all four new checks calling the *shipped* CORE: (5) interval recovery (10 clean ratios reduce to the expected heard p:q); (6) voice folding (voice ratio == folded fy/fx, inside ~[1,2], x pinned to 220); (7) detune → a small nonzero beat (0.880 Hz for a near-2:3); (8) no-clip headroom (2·amp·masterGain ≤ 0.9, peak exactly 0.9000). **Headless-Node** against the *actual embedded `runSelfTest()`* (extracted the inline script via `Function`-construct, module-exports the CORE) → 9/9. **Live browser** (agent-browser, served origin :8731, unique session): badge reads `harmonograph verified — 9/9 ✓`; opening figure Fifth 2:3 → readout "— silent —"; clicking Listen → button "Listening" + ".on" highlight + readout "2:3 · perfect fifth" + expected voices 220/330 Hz; Octave preset → "1:2 · octave"; Surprise → a fresh interval ("3:2 · perfect fifth"); with `ws:pref:muted=1` on reload → button "🔇 Muted", readout "— muted —", silent; muted-user Listen click → unmutes (`ws:pref:muted`→"0") + plays. **Zero console errors** across Listen/Surprise/three preset switches (verified with a `console.error` hook). Screenshot reviewed — the green Listening pill + interval readout sit cleanly in the Motion panel, in-aesthetic.

The lede + bottom hint now teach the audible dimension and link the Sound Garden; the `harmonograph/` CHANGELOG gets a v1.1 entry. `forge --check --all` unaffected (the harmonograph is hand-authored, not a `.src.html` artifact). > **▶ BUILD — The Lattice: from one atom to a CRYSTAL — the 6th Quantum-drift bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=3`, fuel≈9). The bound-state trilogy (Box · Oscillator · Finite Well) had answered "one trapped particle" three model ways; the obvious *bigger* next move — named in NOTES/ROADMAP/`physics-lab-plan.md` §2 — was to take the **next step up in scale: a periodic crystal**, where band structure (and with it the metal/semiconductor/insulator distinction) is born. Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree): one self-contained vanilla HTML file, `cavern/lattice/index.html`, ~640 lines.
> - **Why this seed.** It is the single most natural continuation of the Finite Well: take that one finite well and **repeat it forever**. The discrete bound-state ladder of one atom cannot survive being copied infinitely — it **smears into bands** of allowed energy with **forbidden gaps** between them. That is *the* foundational result of solid-state physics, and it explains why some solids conduct and others don't. It also crosses back to the trilogy: in the deep limit the bands collapse onto the isolated-well levels, so the wing's benches now form a coherent arc (∞ box → bowl → finite well → **crystal**).
> - **The physics (the Kronig–Penney model; ħ=m=1; eV are a fixed display labelling).** A periodic Dirac comb `V(x) = U·Σδ(x−n·a)`, period `a`, dimensionless strength `P = U·a`. Between barriers the particle is free (`k=√(2E)`); Bloch's theorem + the δ-matching across one period gives the **dispersion relation** `cos(q·a) = cos(k·a) + P·sin(k·a)/(k·a) ≡ f(E)`, where `q` is the **crystal momentum**. Since `cos(qa) ∈ [−1,1]`, an energy is **allowed** (a real propagating `q` exists) only where `|f(E)| ≤ 1` — those intervals are the **bands**; the rest, `|f| > 1`, are **forbidden gaps**. Band edges are exactly `f = ±1`, i.e. `qa = nπ`.
> - **The two limits (the soul).** `P → ∞` (atoms far apart): each band **narrows onto an isolated atomic level** `Eₙ = n²π²/(2a²)` — the crystal dissolves back into separate wells (verified: band-1 top edge → `π²/(2a²)` to rel 0.00%, width 1.74 → 0.049 as `P: 8 → 400`). `P → 0` (free electron): the **gaps slam shut** to a continuous `E = q²/2` (gap above band 1: 8.28 → 1.81 → 0.04 as `P: 8 → 1 → 0.02`). The prose draws the punchline: a **half-filled band → metal**, a band **filled exactly to a gap → insulator**, a thin gap → **semiconductor** — *why matter is the way it is.*
> - **The spine = TWO independent cross-checks** (Cavern discipline: closed form vs a different algebra). (1) the **transfer matrix** `M = F·D` over one cell (free-drift matrix `F = [[cos ka, sin ka/k],[−k sin ka, cos ka]]` × δ-kick `D = [[1,0],[2U,1]]`) gives `½·tr M(E) == f(E)` to **4.4e-16** over 4000 energies, and `det M = 1` (the flow is symplectic) to **8.9e-16** — a 2×2 matrix product arriving at the identical dispersion. (2) a **from-scratch eigensolve of a finite ring of N cells** — deflated inverse-power iteration on a *cyclic-tridiagonal* (Sherman–Morrison-wrapped Thomas) solve of the periodic Hamiltonian — puts **exactly N states in each band** (N=8 ring → 8 states in band 1, 8 in band 2), the one-state-per-atom counting law that underlies metals vs insulators. I pre-validated all of this in throwaway Node scripts (`/tmp/kp-check*.mjs`) before writing the bench.
> - **UI.** Two views: the **dispersion** `f(E)` graph (the violet curve threading the green `|f|≤1` corridor; bands shaded green, gaps amber) and the **band diagram** `E(q)` over the reduced zone `q: 0 → π/a` (the textbook plot — band curves rising, forbidden gaps as amber strips). Band chips (one per band — the count is the live headline), `P` (barrier strength) + `a` (spacing) sliders, a periodic-potential overlay sketch, and a readout with the live dispersion-vs-transfer-matrix |Δ| residual.
> - **Self-test 8/8** — proven two ways. Headless-Node against the *actual embedded `runSelfTest()`* (extracted the inline script, stubbed document/localStorage/window via a Proxy) AND live in-browser (agent-browser, served origin :8743, clean console, badge ✓ 8/8, both views + a 390×844 mobile layout screenshot reviewed). The 8 checks: dispersion `f(E) == ½·tr M(E)` & `det M = 1` (4.4e-16 / 8.9e-16) · band edges land on `|f| = 1` (8.5e-14) · gaps truly forbidden `|f| > 1` · free-limit gap → 0 · **atomic-limit band 1 → `π²/(2a²)` (rel 0.00%)** · **N atoms → N states per band (independent ring eigensolve)** · `E(q)` spans the band, `f(E)=cos(qa)` round-trips (1.1e-9) · deterministic.
> - **Integration.** 6th Quantum-drift card in `cavern/index.html` (⛓️) + 2 symmetric self-test checks → Cavern landing **23/23 → 25/25** (verified live in BOTH locked and unlocked states — the unlocked screenshot shows the opened violet Quantum Drift with the new Lattice card). Drops `ws:seen:lattice` (bench-internal breadcrumb, not a front-door PLACES id; `forge --audit-seen` still 13/13). `forge --check --all` clean (29 files). `builds 3→4` → **next session PLANs** (the gauge tips at `builds ≥ 4`). The Quantum Drift is now **six benches deep**; the wing is **nine** overall.
> - **A fresh agent:** the gauge now reads **PLAN** — survey the grounds, sow/curate seeds in [ROADMAP.md](../ROADMAP.md), check metagame health (per [DESIGNING.md](../DESIGNING.md)); don't reflexively build. The Cavern wing remains multi-session if BUILD returns (natural Q-nexts: a **hydrogen radial Coulomb** ladder, a **1-D scattering / wave-packet** bench; plus more Newtonian/Einsteinian per `physics-lab-plan.md` §2).

> **▶ BUILD — The Finite Well: the 5th Quantum-drift bench, completing the bound-state trilogy (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=2`, fuel≈9), and the freshest thread it named — across NOTES.md, ROADMAP.md, and `physics-lab-plan.md` §3 — was a **finite square well** ("a bound ladder *and* a continuum, tails leaking out of a finite-depth box"). Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree): one self-contained vanilla HTML file, `cavern/finite-well/index.html`, ~620 lines.
> - **Why this seed.** It is the third corner of the bound-state trilogy and the *realistic* one. The Box (last-last session) has infinite hard walls and an infinite uneven ladder ∝ n²; the Oscillator (last session) has a soft parabolic bowl with an infinite even ladder ∝ n+½. The finite well sits between them: a box of **finite depth** with climbable walls. Two things the infinite box could never show appear at once — the ladder becomes **finite** (a real well holds only a handful of states), and the wave **leaks out** through the walls and decays. Three benches, three model shapes of a trapped particle.
> - **The physics (ħ=m=1; eV are a fixed display labelling).** Inside |x|<a the wave oscillates with k=√(2E); outside it's an exponential decay κ=√(2(V₀−E)). Matching ψ and ψ′ at the wall x=a quantizes E through a **transcendental** condition — *no closed form*. With u=ka, v=κa on the circle u²+v²=R² (R=√(2V₀)·a, the "well-strength radius"): even-parity states satisfy v=u·tan u, odd-parity v=−u·cot u. The number of bound states is **⌊R/(π/2)⌋+1** — finite, and always ≥1 (a 1-D well always binds). The spine finds each rung by **bisection** on the parity branch ∩ the circle.
> - **The design — let the new physics drive the controls.** The headline interaction is the **depth V₀ slider** (and a **half-width a slider**): as you change them the rung count changes live, and the n-chips rebuild — drag depth toward the floor and watch the top rungs *evaporate*, deepen it and watch them *snap in*. The drawn picture is a finite-depth box (cool-blue climbable walls, an open top = the rim; above the rim is the unbound continuum) with the **finite ladder gauge** showing only the rungs that exist, the |ψ|² hump bulging out past the walls, **wall-crossing dots** marking where the exponential tail begins, a ψ/|ψ|² toggle that dots the n nodes, and a "shade the leak beyond the walls" toggle. The readout headlines the rung energy (below the rim), the **count of bound states**, the depth-below-rim, and the leak %.
> - **The independent spine + an honest numerics call.** The self-test discretizes the **actual stepped potential** −½∂² + V(x) on a grid and pulls the rungs **from scratch by inverse-power iteration** (Thomas tridiagonal solve + Rayleigh quotient) — a genuinely different algebra than the transcendental match. **Key honesty note:** the hard potential **step** makes a uniform-grid FD solve converge at **first order O(h)**, *not* O(h²) like the smooth oscillator — so I did NOT fake a machine-precision claim. I developed the core as a standalone Node module first (`/tmp/fw-core.mjs` + `fw-test.mjs`), saw the raw O(h) convergence (N=800 2.3e-1 → N=1600 1.8e-2), and wrote the test to assert what is *actually true*: the error **shrinks as the grid refines** and the **per-rung relative error is 0.13%** (well under 1%). That's the correct, falsifiable claim for a stepped potential — corroboration, not a lie.
> - **Self-test 8/8 — proven two ways.** (a) headless-Node by **extracting the page's actual inline `runSelfTest()`** (regex out the `<script>`, stub document/localStorage/window/canvas so `draw()` no-ops, call `window.__fw.runSelfTest()`) → 8/8; (b) live in agent-browser (uniquely-named session, served origin :8742, `?v=N` cache-bust): badge shows `self-test ✓ 8/8`, **clean console**, breadcrumb `ws:seen:finite-well` written, screenshots reviewed (default ground state bulging out of the well; a shallow V0=20 well showing only 5 rungs; the deep well's top rung n=12 with signed ψ packing 12 nodes + the red leak-shading on both flanks). The 8 checks: **finite ladder count** ⌊R/(π/2)⌋+1 (V0=20→5, 1.5→2, 200→13, 0.01→1, always ≥1) · every rung **solves the transcendental match + lies on u²+v²=R²** (6.8e-14) · **ψ & ψ′/ψ continuous across the wall** (2.9e-13) · **node theorem** (n-th state has n nodes) · **from-scratch FD eigensolve → the transcendental ladder** (rel 0.13%, O(h) for the stepped V) · **the wave leaks out, shallowest rung farthest** (n=0 0.6% < n=4 80.9%) · **box recovery** V₀→∞ ⇒ ladder → the infinite box (n+1)²π²/(8a²) (rel 3.2e-4) · deterministic. The reference well (V0=20,a=1) ladder: E = 0.92, 3.65, 8.09, 14.00, 19.97 — the top rung at 19.97 sits *just* below the rim V₀=20 and leaks **80.9%**: it barely clings on.
> - **Integration.** 5th Quantum-drift card in `cavern/index.html` (🕳️ "The Finite Well") + **2 symmetric self-test checks** (Finite-Well link present-iff-unlocked + relative) → Cavern landing **21/21 → 23/23**, verified headless AND in both **locked** (no breadcrumbs → 23/23, quantum-open=false) and **unlocked** (walked both drifts → 23/23, quantum-open=true, fwLink present) browser states. Drops `ws:seen:finite-well` (bench-internal, not a front-door PLACES id — like oscillator). `forge --check --all` clean (29 files; the bench is hand-authored, not a forge artifact), `--audit-seen` still 13/13. `cavern/CHANGELOG.md` + this block carry the full provenance. The bound-state trilogy is complete — the Quantum Drift now holds Double Slit · Tunnelling · **Box · Oscillator · Finite Well**. Gauge: `builds 2→3`; fuel unchanged (the GRAND Cavern wing seed stays live — more benches remain). Next session **BUILDs** again (PLAN when builds≥4).

> **▶ BUILD — The Harmonic Oscillator: the 4th Quantum-drift bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=1`, fuel≈9), and the freshest thread it named was "a finite-well / harmonic-oscillator Q-bench where the ladder is *uneven*." Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree): one self-contained vanilla HTML file, `cavern/oscillator/index.html`, ~600 lines.
> - **Why this seed.** It's the deliberate **foil** to the Box I shipped last session, and the contrast is the whole point — quantization survives, but its *character* changes. The roadmap's Physics-Lab seed and `physics-lab-plan.md` §3 both named it the natural next Q-bench. The Box has hard infinite walls and an uneven `n²` ladder; the oscillator has a **soft parabolic bowl** `V=½ω²x²` (a quantum spring) with a **perfectly EVEN** ladder `E_n=ω(n+½)` and wavefunctions that **leak past the classical turning points**. Three crisp differences a visitor can see side-by-side: even ladder vs n², leaking tails vs hard zero at the walls, n nodes vs n−1.
> - **The physics (ħ=m=1; eV are a fixed display labelling).** Physicists' Hermite polynomials by the stable upward recurrence `H_{k+1}=2xH_k−2kH_{k−1}`; the closed eigenstate `ψ_n=(ω/π)^¼/√(2ⁿn!)·H_n(√ω x)·e^{−ωx²/2}`; the classically-forbidden tail `∫_{|x|>x_turn}|ψ_n|²` with `x_turn=√((2n+1)/ω)`; a **coherent state** `|α⟩` (displaced ground state, `c_n=e^{−α²/2}αⁿ/√(n!)`) evolved as `Σ c_n ψ_n e^{−iE_n t}`.
> - **The spine = an independent solve.** The self-test discretizes `−½d²/dx²+½ω²x²` on a truncated line into a symmetric tridiagonal matrix and pulls the lowest eigenvalues **from scratch by inverse-power iteration** (a Thomas solve per step — variable diagonal here because the potential varies — + a Rayleigh quotient), a *different algebra* than Hermite's formula; it converges to `ω(n+½)` as the grid refines (N=200 3.6e−3 → N=1400 7.5e−5, O(h²)). So the even ladder is corroborated, not asserted.
> - **The bench.** Even-ladder gauge with an ℏω spacing bracket; rung chips **0…7** (HO starts at 0); a |ψ|²/signed-ψ toggle (signed mode dots the n nodes); a **"show the classically-forbidden tail"** toggle that shades `|x|>x_turn` red + marks the turning points; an ω stiffness slider; and the crown jewel, a **"Launch a coherent state & let it swing"** button — a rigid, un-spreading Gaussian packet that oscillates `⟨x⟩=x₀cos ωt` like a classical marble (the closest a quantum thing comes to classical). Cold electric-violet `--q` accent + a warm `--bowl` replacing the box's barred-red walls.
> - **Self-test 8/8 — proven two ways.** (a) **Headless Node against the *actual embedded* `runSelfTest()`** (extracted the inline `<script>`, stubbed document/localStorage/window/raf) and (b) **live in-browser** (agent-browser, served origin `:8731`, clean console, screenshots reviewed). The eight: **even ladder** `E_n=ω(n+½)` (max|gap−ω|=0, E_0=½>0) · each ψ_n **solves the Schrödinger ODE** (FD residual 1.5e−7) · **orthonormal** ⟨ψ_m|ψ_n⟩=δ_mn (2.3e−11) · **node theorem: n nodes** (box's is n−1) · **from-scratch FD eigensolve → even ladder** O(h²) · **wave leaks past the walls, leak shrinks with n** (n=0 15.7%==theory 15.73% > n=1 11.2% > n=5 7.4%) · **coherent state swings classically** ⟨x⟩=x₀cos ωt to 3.3e−15, norm=1 to 3.5e−15 · deterministic.
> - **The test earned its keep — caught a real bug.** First node-counter used the naive `prev*cur<0` scan; it **missed the x=0 node of the odd states** (ψ₇ → counted 6, not 7) when a grid sample landed exactly on x=0 (ψ=−0 there ⇒ `0·anything=0`, not `<0`). Root-cause fix: track the running **non-zero** sign so a zero straddled by ± registers as exactly one crossing — principled, not a tolerance fudge. Went 7/8 → 8/8.
> - **Browser-verified states (screenshots reviewed):** ground state = single Gaussian hump in the warm bowl; **n=5** = 5 dotted nodes + 6-hump |ψ|² piling up near the turning points + 7.4% forbidden tail with the wave poking into the red shaded zone; **coherent state** = an identical-shaped packet that visibly slid **left→right of center** between two frames 1.2s apart (⟨x⟩ marker tracking `x₀cos ωt`), never spreading.
> - **Integration:** 4th Quantum-drift card in `cavern/index.html` (🌀, green proof badge) + 2 symmetric self-test checks → Cavern landing tally **19/19 → 21/21**, verified headless in **both** the locked and unlocked states. Drops `ws:seen:oscillator` (bench-internal like `box`, not a front-door PLACES id, so `--audit-seen` unaffected at 13/13). `forge --check --all` clean (29 files current). **Quantum Drift now four benches deep; Cavern seven benches overall.** Gauge: `builds 1→2`, fuel unchanged (the GRAND Cavern wing seed stays live — more benches remain). Committed + pushed to `main`.

> **▶ BUILD — Particle in a Box: the 3rd Quantum-drift bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **BUILD** (`builds=0`, fuel≈9), and the freshest thread it named was "a 3rd Quantum bench — Particle-in-a-Box." Built entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree): one self-contained vanilla HTML file, `cavern/box/index.html`, ~470 lines.
> - **Why this seed.** It's the *foundation* of the wing's quantum story (the Double Slit shows superposition, Tunnelling shows barrier penetration; the Box shows *quantization itself* — why energy comes in a ladder, why atoms have lines not a smear). `worklog/physics-lab-plan.md` §3 named it the natural next one, and the Cavern CHANGELOG had explicitly **deferred** it — so I closed that loop.
> - **The physics (ħ=m=1, box on [0,1]; eV/nm are a fixed display labelling).** Infinite square well: V=0 inside, V=∞ at two walls ⇒ ψ must vanish at the walls ⇒ a discrete ladder `E_n = n²π²/2`, `ψ_n = √2·sin(nπx)` with exactly n half-waves and **n−1 interior nodes**, no rung at zero (zero-point energy). Plus a time-dependent superposition `Ψ(x,t)=Σ c_n ψ_n e^{−iE_n t}`.
> - **The independent solve — the bench's spine (the workshop's "corroborate, don't assert" discipline).** The self-test discretizes the Schrödinger operator `−½ d²/dx²` with Dirichlet walls into a symmetric tridiagonal matrix and finds its lowest eigenvalues **from scratch by inverse-power iteration** — a Thomas tridiagonal solve per step + a Rayleigh quotient, a genuinely *different algebra* than the n²π²/2 formula. It agrees with the discrete-Toeplitz theory to **8.5e−14**, and the discrete eigenvalues converge to the analytic ladder as the grid refines (rel err N=40 8.2e−3 → N=1600 5.1e−6, clean O(h²)). Same spirit as Tunnelling's transfer-matrix cross-check and the Singing Plate's Lanczos eigensolver.
> - **Self-test 8/8** — proven two ways: (a) **headless in Node** by extracting the inline `<script>` body from the shipped HTML and running the *actual* `runSelfTest()` (stubbing `document`/`localStorage`) → 8/8; (b) **live in-browser** (agent-browser, served origin, clean console) → "self-test ✓ 8/8". The eight checks: (1) ladder `E_n/E_1=n²` exact n=1..8, E_1>0; (2) orthonormality `⟨ψ_m|ψ_n⟩=δ_mn` to **1.55e−15** (analytic vs an independent Simpson integral); (3) **node theorem** n−1 interior nodes n=1..7; (4) FD eigenvalues→closed ladder O(h²); (5) from-scratch inverse-power == FD theory **8.5e−14**; (6) time evolution conserves probability ∫|Ψ|²=1 to **2.5e−15**; (7) **selection rule** — ⟨x⟩ swings 0.357 for a *mixed-parity* (1+2) superposition but is **frozen at ½** (4.9e−15) for same-parity (1+3), the quantum origin of which transitions emit light; (8) deterministic (pure core).
> - **The bench (browser-verified, screenshots reviewed).** Left: an **energy-ladder gauge** (rungs n=1..8, the live rung glows violet, energies climbing as n²). Centre: the box between two red `V=∞` walls, showing the selected rung's `|ψ|²` probability hump + an optional signed ψ with its **interior nodes dotted**. A **width-L** slider sinks/raises the whole ladder (roomier box = calmer). A **Mix** button superposes rungs 1+2 and *evolves* it — the bound packet visibly **sloshes** side to side, a live gold ⟨x⟩ marker tracking its centre. Verified *animating*: ⟨x⟩ moved right→left between two captured frames; n=4 showed 4 humps + 3 nodes + "16 = 4²"; ground state read 0.376 eV / 0 nodes. Matches the Quantum-drift's violet template (same shell as Tunnelling). Drops `ws:seen:box`.
> - **Integration.** Added a 3rd bench card to the Quantum Drift in `cavern/index.html` (📦, proof badge "ladder E_n ∝ n² == a from-scratch eigensolve · orthonormal to 1e−15") + **two symmetric self-test checks** (box link present-iff-unlocked + relative) → the Cavern landing self-test is now **19/19** (was 17/17). Browser-verified end-to-end: a fresh origin keeps the drift `hidden`; seeding a Newtonian + Einsteinian breadcrumb (`walkedBothDrifts`) reveals the drift with all **three** Q-benches and the box card links `box/index.html` (relative). `forge --check --all` clean (no drift; the box page isn't a forge artifact); `forge --audit-seen` still **13/13** (the `box` breadcrumb is bench-internal, not a front-door PLACES id, so correctly not required there). **The Quantum Drift is now three benches deep; the Cavern wing is six benches overall.**

> **▶ TENDING THE TOOLING BED — the breadcrumb-plumbing fix + `forge --audit-seen` (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The gauge read **PLAN** (builds≥4), so this ran the gardener mode — but instead of a prose-only pass I bloomed one curation seed end-to-end, because it tends the *tooling* bed and the workshop's whole exploration layer depends on it. Done entirely myself in-turn (no deputy/worktree).
> - **The latent bug the survey turned up.** The front-door estate map drops `WS.seen(id)` on a POI **click** (`index.src.html` ~line 962), so map navigation lights the Survey-of-Heaven sky and feeds the Undercroft's earned secrets. But a survey of all 13 PLACES target pages found **9 of them never dropped their own `ws:seen:<id>`** — so a **direct visit / deep-link / bookmark** (e.g. someone landing on `firmament/index.html` from a search result, never touching the map) registered **nothing**. The Survey's sky-fill and any visit-gated Undercroft secret silently under-counted those arrivals. The "OK" pages (verse, cartographer, physics-lab/cavern, undercroft) already inlined ws.js and called `WS.seen('<id>')` at parse time — exactly the convention cartographer's own comment documents ("*A converted page only has to (1) inline ws.js and (2) call WS.seen('<id>')*"). The other 9 (compositor, sound-garden, threshold, strange-garden, firmament, hall-of-mirrors, daedalus, arcade, workbench) never got that wiring.
> - **The root-cause fix.** Rather than inline the full 345-line ws.js nine times (it self-inits with chimes + unlock-checking, which the front door / Undercroft already re-derive from the breadcrumbs), I added a **tiny self-contained breadcrumb shim** before each page's closing `</body>` — faithful to `WS.seen`'s actual breadcrumb write: `try{ if(localStorage['ws:seen:<id>']==null) localStorage['ws:seen:<id>']=Date.now() }catch(e){}`. Confirmed against `tools/ws/ws.js` lines 38-42 that this *is* exactly what `WS.seen` does for the key; the heavier machinery (bootstrap/checkUnlocks/chime) runs on the front door + Undercroft, which re-read the keys. Inserted via a small Node patcher (`/tmp/patch-seen.mjs`) that targets the *last* `</body>` and is a no-op if the page already drops its id. **All 13 PLACES pages now drop their breadcrumb on a direct visit.**
> - **The health-monitor (the seed's literal ask — ROADMAP §curation).** Built **`forge --audit-seen [root] [--strict]`** in `tools/forge/forge.mjs`: it parses the `(id, href)` pairs straight out of `index.src.html`'s PLACES array (regex over the array body — so it stays correct as the estate grows, no hardcoded id→folder map), resolves each href to its on-disk page, and checks the file contains the literal `ws:seen:<id>`. **Soft by default** (warns + exit 0, per the seed's "soft warning"); **`--strict` exits 1** so a CI gate can adopt it. Added USAGE + a factored `auditSeen()`/`parsePlaces()`.
> - **Falsifiability (the workshop's discipline — a check must be able to fail).** Temporarily stripped arcade's shim → the audit **⚠-warned and listed arcade**, soft mode still exited 0, **`--strict` exited 1**; restoring the shim → **13/13 green, exit 0** again. So the monitor genuinely detects a regression, not just rubber-stamps. (Also: `forge --check --all` stays clean — none of the 9 patched pages are forge artifacts, so no generated-file drift.)
> - **Browser verification (served origin :8731, agent-browser, unique session).** Direct-loaded `firmament/index.html` (a former NO-DROP page) → `localStorage` afterwards held `["ws:seen:firmament"]`; then direct-loaded `daedalus/index.html` in the same session → both `ws:seen:firmament` and `ws:seen:daedalus` present (shared origin, persisted). Patched `hall-of-mirrors/index.html` (which has its own unlock script) rendered clean with `ws:seen:hall-of-mirrors` set and its title intact — no console breakage from the added shim. Test breadcrumbs lived only in a throwaway agent-browser session, not Brandon's profile.
> - **The gardener pass (the PLAN-mode duties).** Pruned the bloomed forge-check seed (struck through with its provenance). Sowed **2 fresh `exhibit` seeds** — **The Harmonograph** (damped twin-pendulum Lissajous/rose figures; feeds the existing Harmonograph×Sound-Garden cross) and **A Catenary bench** (the hanging chain is a `cosh`, falsifiable: catenary beats any parabola fit on potential energy) — and **1 fresh `cross`**: "**Cavern × Survey of Heaven — The Experimenter**", promoted from a bare metagame "consideration" because this very plumbing fix makes a Cavern feats-constellation cheap and reliable (direct visits now register). Refreshed the metagame table (the Undercroft + Survey rows both note the hardening; the Experimenter row points at the new seed). **Reset the gauge: `builds-since-plan = 0` → next session BUILDs**; fuel ≈ 9.
> - **Docs:** this block + the INDEX line; ROADMAP seedbed (prune + 3 sows + table refresh + gauge); NOTES current-state replaced + gauge line rewritten. **No hidden piece** was built or touched — the 🗝️ inventory is unchanged; this was infrastructure that *strengthens* the existing metagames (the Undercroft/Survey both read `ws:seen`), not a new secret.

> **▶ THE QUANTUM DRIFT DEEPENS — Quantum Tunnelling, the drift's second bench (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** A focused single-bench BUILD. The Cavern's Quantum Drift (opened the prior session) had **one** bench (the Double Slit) while the Newtonian and Einsteinian drifts each have **two** — so the deepest, strangest drift felt thin. The head-gardener call: give it a true second bench rather than start a new wing. Built **Quantum Tunnelling** entirely myself in-turn (no deputy, no worktree — the `/fun` rules now require one in-turn build+commit+push), browser-verified on a served origin, integrated, committed, pushed.
> - **The bench** (`cavern/tunnelling/index.html`, **712 lines**, Quantum drift) ⛰️ — a particle rolls at a rectangular barrier it hasn't the energy to climb. Classically it always reflects; quantum-mechanically the wavefunction decays through the wall and a sliver transmits. The canvas renders the **iconic three regions** over an energy-level diagram: incident wave + reflection ripples (violet) on the left, **exponential evanescent decay** inside the barred red (classically-forbidden) wall, and the small transmitted travelling wave on the far side; the dashed **E** line sits below the **V₀** barrier top (and rises above it in the over-barrier regime, where the curve turns green and shows resonance ripples). Controls: Energy E (as a fraction of V₀), barrier height V₀, width L, a **Send a wavepacket** animation (a glowing blob crosses, shrinking through the wall), and a **Find a resonance** button that jumps E to the first `qL=π` transparency.
> - **Self-test 7/7 — cross-checked in Node first, then verified identical in the browser**, falsifiable: (1) **E < V₀ tunnels** — `0 < T < 1` where classical physics demands exactly **0** (T = 0.420 at E = ½V₀); (2) the textbook **closed form `T = 1/(1 + V₀²sinh²(κL)/(4E(V₀−E)))`** (analytically branch-continued to `sin` for E > V₀, and to the κ→0 limit `T = 1/(1+V₀L²/2)` at E = V₀) **== an independent 2×2 complex transfer-matrix wave solve** that matches ψ and ψ′ at both walls by a *different algebra* — **max |Δ| = 1.9e−14 over 56 configs** (so the formula is corroborated, not asserted); (3) **unitarity R + T = 1** to **3.3e−16**; (4) **exponential fragility** — thick-wall `d ln T/dL → −2κ` (measured −2.17 vs −2.19), the reason α-decay half-lives span ~24 orders of magnitude; (5) **resonant transparency** — above the barrier, `qL = nπ` ⇒ the wall is **perfectly clear, T = 1** (1.000000000000 at the first resonance); (6) **high-energy** E ≫ V₀ ⇒ wall vanishes, T → 1 (0.999995 at E/V₀ = 200); (7) **monotone in width** — wider wall strictly tunnels less. Live readout: T, R, R + T (7 digits), regime, decay κ inside the wall, and the closed-form-vs-numeric |Δ| (1.4e−17 at default). Drops `ws:seen:tunnelling`.
> - **Wired into the drift** (`cavern/index.html`): a second `a.bench` added to `#quantumDrift`. The landing self-test is **unchanged at 17/17** — its bench-enumeration checks are `every`/`allRelative` predicates over the open-drift benches plus a Double-Slit-iff-unlocked check, none of which a *second quantum* bench disturbs (verified, not assumed). The Cavern is plain HTML, so **no forge run** was needed.
> - **Browser verification (served origin, `?v=N` cache-bust, agent-browser):** the **bench** loads clean (0 console errors, canvas 936×577, self-test **7/7**); default state T = 0.1013 tunnelling, R + T = 1.0000000, Δ = 1.4e−17; dragging E above V₀ flips the regime label to "over-barrier" and the **Find a resonance** button lands T = 1.0000. The **integrated Cavern** stays **17/17** in BOTH the locked state (quantum drift hidden, both benches present in DOM) and — after setting `ws:seen:cradle` + `ws:seen:light-clock` and reloading — the **unlocked** state (`quantum-open` true, drift revealed, BOTH "The Double Slit" and "Quantum Tunnelling" visible). Test breadcrumbs were cleared from the served origin afterward.

No hidden piece was built this session (the Quantum *Drift* is a public, in-cave spatial reveal earned by walking both drifts — distinct from the Undercroft's hidden inventory; the 🗝️ callout is untouched).

Self-test **7/7**: **γ from the photon-slant geometry == the closed form** — solved by bisection on T=√(L²+(β·T)²), independent of any 1/√(1−β²) algebra, across 501 β-values, matching to **2.38e-13**; **interval s²=t²−x² invariant** across 4000 random events × Lorentz boosts (|Δs²|<1e-12, tightest 3.11e-13); velocity addition strictly < c (5000 sub-c pairs); boost∘inverse-boost == identity (4.48e-13); length contraction L/γ measured from boosted rod endpoints (7.44e-15); **falsifiable control** — the Galilean transform wrecks the interval (error 7.23e+1 ≫ tol, so the invariance is a real constraint, not a number compared to itself); limits & monotonicity (γ(0)=1 exactly; γ rises strictly with β). At 0.99c the rest clock shows **21 ticks vs the moving clock's 3** — time dilation made visceral. Responsive to 760px; drops `ws:seen:light-clock`. Committed on `bench-light-clock`.
>   - **Newton's Cradle** ⚙️ (`cavern/cradle/`, 999 lines, Newtonian) — the swinging-brass-ball toy (3–7 balls, lift-k stepper, restitution + unequal-mass sliders, drag/click-to-lift, live momentum/KE/total-E readout). Self-test **6/6**: **elastic pairwise collision conserves p AND KE** to <1e-12 over 4000 random pairs (tightest **5.68e-14**); **lift k ⇒ exactly k swing out** for all N∈[3,7], k∈[1,N−1] at e=1 — the whole row's p & KE conserved across the multi-collision event (the cradle theorem, the hard one); **inelastic is honest** — at e<1, p stays conserved but KE strictly decreases, never rises; equal-mass head-on swaps velocities exactly; **falsifiable control** — the naive "both balls just stop" rule is caught as KE-destroying; **unequal masses at e=1** break the tidy "k out" rule yet both ledgers stay closed (<1e-9). Live: pulling e down to 0.75 stair-steps total-E 3.13 → 0.63 while momentum stays conserved at every click. Responsive at 740px; drops `ws:seen:cradle`. Committed `c2f60e8` on `bench-cradle`.
>   - **Mercury's Precession** ☿ (`cavern/precession/`, 846 lines, Einsteinian) — the crispest GR test there is: one famous number to hit. **Integrates the relativistic Binet orbit `u″ = −u + GM/h² + (3GM/c²)u²` (RK4)** into a walking rosette; a Newton/Einstein toggle (Newton = a closed warm ellipse; Einstein = the cold-blue precessing rosette with marching gold perihelion marks), a **visual-exaggeration slider ×1→×1M** that amplifies the *drawing* while the numbers + self-test use the **true, un-exaggerated** physics, plus a planet picker and e/a sliders. The headline reads **42.98 ″/century**, Mercury's 43″ called out. *(The deputy prototyped the perihelion-detection in Node first — caught an aphelion-vs-perihelion crossing bug — before wiring it in.)* Committed `d5c5d34` on `bench-precession`.

> **▶ THE BRACHISTOCHRONE — the curve of fastest descent, and its tautochrone twin (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed. The 5th and final build of the autonomous run.** A clean self-contained Workbench piece (deliberately NOT the grand Physics Lab wing — that's fleshed and saved for a focused session). Bernoulli's 1696 problem made playable.
> - **The piece** (`brachistochrone/index.html`, 910 lines): two modes. **Race** — four beads released from A slide frictionlessly to B down a straight line, a circular arc, a parabola, and the gold **cycloid**, each moving by real energy-conservation speed `v=√(2g·drop)`; the cycloid wins (a live finishing-order leaderboard). **Tautochrone** — five beads dropped from five heights on one cycloid cup all reach the bottom together at `T=π√(r/g)`. Sliders for B + g; 3 skins (Slate/Blueprint/Parchment).
> - **Self-test 7/7 — verified in Node AND browser (matching):** descent times **cycloid 0.8056 s < parabola 0.8239 < arc 0.8289 < line 1.0096** (the cycloid wins by 0.0186 s); cycloid numeric vs analytic √(r/g)·θB **|Δ|=1.11e-16**; genuine minimum (all 5 perturbations *increase* the time); **tautochrone** — 6 release heights arrive with **spread 0.00e+0**, max |T−π√(r/g)| **9.44e-15**; **falsifiable** — a circular cup is NOT tautochrone (spread 0.048 s, the pendulum's amplitude dependence); deterministic. *Honest caveat:* the cycloid/tautochrone times are machine-precision (constant integrand / exact substitution), but the line/arc/parabola race times are a numerical integral whose 1/√y release singularity is regularized by an `x=s²` substitution — convergent to ~9 digits (N≈2000), far tighter than the 0.0186 s margin needs.
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy verified the numerics headlessly in Node FIRST (`core.mjs`/`race2.mjs`/`tauto2.mjs` — good rigor for the tricky singularity), then built + browser-verified (race mid-run, tautochrone convergence, the 7/7 panel, mobile-380, a skin). Lead reviewed the race screenshot (gold cycloid diving steep then coasting to win — pedagogically crisp + on-brand) and cleaned a stray screenshot the deputy had momentarily dropped in the repo root. Lead added the Workbench card (Toys & benches, glyph 🛝 — a slide — after the Straightedge). Repo dir ship-clean.
> - **Docs:** NOTES current-state (rewritten to a clean-rest run summary) + a new project-status row; ROADMAP Brachistochrone seed pruned (bloomed same day); gauge → `fuel=7 · builds-since-plan=2`. **Run concluded after this** — heartbeat retired, clean rest. > **▶ FOURIER EPICYCLES — draw anything with rotating circles (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The 4th build of the autonomous run, and the first to grow a seed sown *that same session* (the PLAN session sowed `[exhibit] Fourier epicycles`; the next build pulled it — the seedbed loop closing in real time). A clean, self-contained Workbench piece chosen deliberately to NOT preempt the freshly-fleshed Physics Lab wing (which deserves a focused session).
> - **The piece** (`epicycles/index.html`, 975 lines): any closed curve is a sum of **rotating vectors**. A genuine **complex DFT** (implemented from scratch, no FFT lib) over M=256 sampled path-points → each frequency a circle (radius |c[k]|, speed k, phase arg c[k]); chained tip-to-tail and ordered by amplitude they re-trace the curve with a gold pen over a faint dashed target. An **N slider (1→256)** sweeps from one wobbly circle to the exact figure. Six presets (Star/Heart/Treble/Letter A/Gear/Infinity) + working **freehand mouse-draw** (arc-length resampled, loop-closed); play/pause/speed; show-toggles; 3 skins (Midnight/Blueprint/Ember). House aesthetic (cool circles, warm-gold pen).
> - **Self-test 6/6 — verified in Node AND in-browser (matching numbers):** round-trip `idft(dft(z))=z` max err **2.18e-12**; full rotating-vector reconstruction at the sample times **1.21e-12**; top-N RMS error **monotonically decreasing** (74.7 @N=1 → 30.3 @N=32 → **9.89e-13** @N=M); **Parseval** `Σ|z|²/M==Σ|c|²` (an *independent* identity, not the round-trip) rel err **3.25e-15**; deterministic; **falsifiable** (amplitude ordering strictly beats bin ordering at N=8 [66.6 vs 75.1]; dropping a term raises the N=M−1 error from ~1e-12 to ~0.5).
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy built + browser-verified (star mid-anim, low-N-vs-high-N sharpening, nested-circle heart, freehand, mobile-380, the 6/6 panel), leaving a Node test-core in `/tmp`. Lead did the aesthetic QA on the screenshot (clean, on-brand, the "verified 6/6" chip) — the deputy's Node+browser cross-validation stood in for a lead re-run (the core was a function module without a main). Lead added the Workbench card (Toys & benches, glyph 🌀, beside the Harmonograph — kin drawing-machines). Repo dir ship-clean.

> **▶ THE PLANIMETER — a working polar (Amsler) area-measuring instrument (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The 3rd build of the autonomous run; deliberately broke the optics streak for variety (pulled the planimeter half of the `[exhibit] Planimeter / nomograph` seed; the nomograph half stays in the bed). The estate's first **mechanical-integration** instrument, joining the Workbench's "real instruments that do real math" vein (slide rule, astrolabe, abacus). Trace a closed boundary and a measuring **wheel** — axle along the tracer arm, rolling only on motion *perpendicular* to the arm — records the enclosed **area** as `Area = L·ΔW` (Green's theorem in brass). Real forward kinematics (pole → pole-arm → elbow → tracer-arm, elbow solved by arm-circle intersection, branch-locked); pick a figure (circle/square/ellipse/polygon) and watch it auto-trace with the linkage articulating + the area climbing, or drag the tracer by hand. Brass/blueprint/boxwood skins, 2× PNG. 1209 lines.
> - **Self-test 7/7 — and genuinely NON-circular** (the requirement I set + independently re-ran in Node myself, identical result): the wheel-roll integral (THE INSTRUMENT — a perpendicular-projection sum along the path) checked against ground truth computed a *different* way (πr²/πab closed forms + the **shoelace formula** for polygons). **Max area rel error 2.03e-7** (a discretized boundary integral, clean **O(1/N²)** convergence — honest: a planimeter is an approximator that sharpens with sampling, not an exact closed form like the optics benches; framed as such). **Pole-independence spread 9.0e-15** — the area is invariant to where the pole is pinned, the *defining* planimeter property, at machine epsilon. Zero-path ~0; falsifiable (wrong arm length L → 5% error); deterministic. **Zero-circle/pole-enclosure handled by avoidance** (pole kept outside the figure → the correction term never arises; `Area=L·ΔW` holds exactly) — disclosed, not hidden.
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy built + browser-verified (circle/polygon/ellipse traces, the 7/7 panel, mobile-380, reduced-motion), leaving its Node test-core in `/tmp` (not the repo). I then **independently re-ran that core in Node** (`runcore.cjs` → 7/7, maxAreaErr 2.028e-7, poleSpread 8.996e-15) rather than trust the screenshot of the self-test panel (which had caught a mid-trace HUD), and did the aesthetic QA on the circle/instrument screenshot myself. Lead added the Workbench card (Instruments group, glyph 📐, after the Abacus — a quick shared-file edit, no deputy). Repo dir ship-clean (index.html + CHANGELOG only).
> - **Docs:** NOTES current-state + a new project-status row; README untouched (Workbench pieces aren't in the README front matter); ROADMAP planimeter-half pruned (nomograph kept); gauge → `fuel=6 · builds-since-plan=3` (**one more build trips a PLAN session** — which will flesh a grand bet: Physics Lab / the Workshop Mystery). Two commits: bench (`a9a9bad`) + Workbench card & docs.

> **▶ THE LIGHTHOUSE — a Fresnel-lens optical bench, the Hall's 12th (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** The 2nd build of the autonomous run; pulled the `[exhibit] Lighthouse Fresnel lens` seed from the bed. A live bench on the **Fresnel lens** — collapse a thick converging lens into concentric annular **prism zones** and it keeps the focus but loses the bulk (the real lighthouse innovation). Three views: the smooth parent lens, the flat saw-tooth Fresnel sheet (rays converging to the common focus), and **Lighthouse mode** (lamp at the focus → a sweeping collimated gold beam, by reciprocity). Sliders for f / rings N / glass index n; three colour skins.
> - **Correctness (the workshop signature):** each zone's facet angle is solved by **exact vector Snell's law** (front slanted facet → flat back, traced air→glass→air, root-found by bisection — no thin-prism/small-angle shortcut), so a parallel bundle focuses to a single point *by construction*. Self-test **5/5** (in-browser, f=2.2, n=1.49): focus convergence **max miss 9.99e-16 m** (≈ float epsilon); reciprocity exact (max exit angle 0 rad); f_eff = 2.2 to 2.2e-15; deterministic (facets bit-identical on recompute); **falsifiable** (a 1e-6 rad facet perturbation misses by ~1000× the tolerance). 876 lines, self-contained, house aesthetic, back-links to Hall + workshop, drops `ws:seen:lighthouse`, **no feat flags touched** (a study bench, not one of the 9 feats — metagame-as-consideration, not mandate). 60fps, clean console, 380px reflow, `prefers-reduced-motion`.
> - **Orchestration:** a build deputy built + browser-verified the standalone bench (I reviewed the parent/fresnel/lighthouse/mobile screenshots + the 5/5 self-test myself), then — since `SendMessage` to the redesign deputy wasn't available in this session — a 2nd fresh deputy READ the just-redesigned Hall index and added a matching **12th card** in the "Rays, lenses & mirrors" band after the Spyglass: glyph 🗼, kind "fresnel lens · lighthouse", a new inline-SVG **Fresnel-rings vignette** (`.v-lighthouse` — concentric ring-arcs + a ray bundle drawing to a focal dot, wired through the existing `.hot` hover listener, distinct from Caustic's bare bundle and the Spyglass's solid lenses), and a hand-interpolated hue (`#ffb989`) slotted between Spyglass and Camera Obscura to keep the red→violet progression. `git diff --numstat` = +29/-0 — purely additive; Feats IIFE + other 11 cards byte-identical. Both deputies browser-verified; I did the aesthetic QA on screenshots.
> - **Docs:** README count ten→eleven optical benches + a Lighthouse bullet; NOTES current-state + project-status table → 12 benches; seed pruned (bloomed); gauge → `fuel=7 · builds-since-plan=2`. Two commits: the bench (`4583c08`) then the Hall card + docs.

> **▶ HALL OF MIRRORS REDESIGN — "The Dispersion: A Prism's Throw" — the FIRST build under the new gardener/builder system (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8, ultracode). Shipped & pushed.** The poetic close to the planning-system chat: the Hall is the room DESIGNING.md names as its *cautionary tale* — a strong-metaphor room (optics) that shipped as a plain vertical card-list. So the first BUILD fixes it, and dogfoods DESIGNING.md's headline rule (*form expresses content; force divergent form concepts before committing*).
> - **The design:** the legible single-column card-spine + full blurbs stay (it's still a directory of 11 benches to compare). Layered on: an inline-SVG **entrance prism** by the title throwing a dispersed fan; a left **dispersion rail** running white (at the prism) → the six wash colours top→bottom; the **11 benches ordered red→violet by how each treats light**; the 4 physics groups restyled as labelled **spectral bands**; a per-card spectral hue-edge bar. **The soul:** each card carries a **live ~40px inline-SVG vignette of its own physics** that animates on hover/focus only (`.hot` class, transform/opacity/stroke-dashoffset, idle CPU zero) — rainbow arc draws, polariser crosses to extinction, prism fans, kaleidoscope spins, Newton's rings shimmer, maze routes to its gem, etc. Caustic seated first as the source the spectrum is thrown from. The `:root` palette, `.card`/`.feat` styles, the spectral `<h1>`, and the whole **Feats-of-Light** IIFE kept verbatim. 311 → **577 lines**, plain page (no forge).
> - **Process (the new pipeline, dogfooded end-to-end):** (1) a **divergence Workflow** `hall-redesign-concepts` — 6 designers, each LOCKED to a distinct optical lens (spectrum / live-ray / walked-corridor / lit-gallery / light-tree / engraved-schematic), blind to each other, → a curator-judge scoring metaphor/legibility/feasibility/distinctiveness/delight/mobile (legibility weighted heavily). Judge ranked: Dispersion 54 (winner, safest-legible) · Splitting-Ray 52 · Hung-Spectrum-gallery 51 · Optician's-Plate 48 · Beamwalk 47 · Long-Gallery 45. The wild concepts (corridor renders only 1 of 11 readable; schematic can't carry 11 blurbs) genuinely failed the *compare-11-benches* test — exactly DESIGNING.md's "collections stay legible." (2) **My curatorial call** (owning the design per [[i-own-all-project-calls]]): take the judge's bulletproof Dispersion spine but graft the gallery concept's **live-vignette soul** so it's not "just a coloured stripe" — that graft is the heart, not chrome. (3) a **build deputy** evolved the file + browser-verified on a served origin: all 11 links + Ripple kin + back-link; Feats 0/3/9 contract (set 3 → 3/9 + right chips lit; set 9 → 9/9 + capstone hint); 380px reflow (scrollWidth===380, no overflow); console clean; **62fps**; contrast unchanged (name=`--ink`, blurb=`--ink-dim`, colour confined to rail/edges/prism/vignettes); `prefers-reduced-motion` honored. I then reviewed the actual screenshots myself (desktop/hover/mobile/feats-9of9) — the aesthetic call is the lead's.
> - **Deputy's good deviations (accepted):** all 11 vignettes animate (none shipped static); on ≤380px the prism is hidden entirely (the h1 already reads as header) rather than a redundant glyph; Feats chip array left byte-identical to guarantee the contract (the warm→cool reorder was optional); the droppable `#stage` scroll-hue-shift was skipped to avoid jank.
> - **Seedbed:** the `[curation] Redesign the Hall` seed BLOOMED (pruned, with a follow-on note: apply the live-vignette/distinctive-form treatment to other *collection* rooms only where a metaphor is genuinely being wasted). Gauge → `fuel=8 · builds-since-plan=1`.
> - **Reflection — the system worked.** The thing that made this NOT a repeat of the original mistake was steps (1)+(2): forcing six blind divergent concepts before committing surfaced the live-vignette idea I'd never have reached by anchoring on the existing list, and the legibility-weighted judge kept the wild ones from winning. That's precisely the rut-breaker the planning system was built to be.

> **▶ THE PLANNING SYSTEM — a gardener/builder workflow so sessions sometimes shape *direction*, not just build the next exhibit (2026-06-13, `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Scaffolding shipped + Brandon's first seeds sown.** A strategic chat with Brandon, not a build. He named three cold-start limits of a context-bounded agent on a now-large project: (1) **metagames get forgotten** — new rooms don't tie into the Undercroft / Survey of Heaven; (2) **repetitive room design** — the Hall of Mirrors copied the front-door's vertical door-list and wasted an optics metaphor; (3) **no high-level plan** — sessions pick the nearest plausible exhibit and never step up to a new room/metagame/engine, or curate. Root cause (his): *I'm trained to follow the user's lead, not my own, and at scale no single context holds the whole estate.*
> - **The synthesis (all three are one root cause → one fix):** make the missing consideration *unavoidable at the moment of decision*, not "another doc to remember." A **gardener** sows; a **builder** grows. Where I pushed back on Brandon (the anti-sycophancy check — agreeing with everything would *be* the bug he named): I'd proposed a *forced forge check* for metagame integration; he rightly softened it (not everything needs every metagame; some are complete) → metagame health became a **curation axis + `kind: metagame` seeds**, with "complete — do not pad" a recordable state. Cadence: his RNG idea → my **stateful gauge** (RNG ignores the real signal); his flat idea-pool → my **two gauges** (exhibit *fuel* vs direction *bets*).
> - **Shipped (3 files):** **`ROADMAP.md`** — the seedbed: typed seeds (`exhibit·room·metagame·engine·curation·cross`) each **≤3 lines** (the "sow don't dictate" rule, structural), bootstrapped from the migrated NOTES next-steps menu + a metagame-health table. **`DESIGNING.md`** — the gardener's charter (survey/sow/curate-incl-**retire**/metagame-health/prune) + the builder's pipeline (**floor-not-ceiling**, **form-expresses-content** [strong-metaphor rooms diverge, collections stay legible], divergence-before-commit, metagame-as-consideration, the one `ws:seen` plumbing rule, definition-of-done). **`NOTES.md`** — a **🎲 mode gauge** section near the top (`PLAN if fuel ≲4 OR builds ≥4; else BUILD` ≈ 1-in-5, no RNG) + a live gauge line; migrated the inline next-steps menu + the stale "fresh thread" list into the seedbed.
> - **Mine, added unprompted:** a 4th observation — *the estate only ever grows; nothing is retired/merged/deepened* → **retire** is now an explicit gardener duty. And **`cross`** as a first-class seed kind (the richest pieces here are crossings — the Undercroft's verse×script, cartographer×scriptorium). The north star, stated in DESIGNING.md: *this is leisure — the lightest structure that breaks the rut, no more.*
> - **Deferred follow-up (sown as a curation seed):** the soft forge `--check` warning for the `ws:seen` plumbing rule (a code change, dogfooding DESIGNING.md's one mechanical rule).
> - **Brandon's first seeds, sown same session** (from his vault notes — substance captured in ROADMAP, his personal vault paths deliberately kept *out* of the public repo). Mostly grand *bets*: **The Workshop Mystery** (a manor-wide clue-hunt across every exhibit — Scriptorium seeds, crypto/star-chart/arcade gates → a fictional manor-history reveal; the grandest, ~3 sessions) · **Physics Lab** (Newtonian + Einsteinian wings + a secret quantum sub-wing earned by exploring both; form idea: a *cave outside the manor*) · **Alchemy Lab** · **Flight & Rocketry** · crosses **Light × Sound** + **ambient music on the front door** (random headless soundscapes from *seen* instruments) · the redesign-the-Hall-of-Mirrors-navigation curation seed (his form nudge = my own cautionary tale). And the **small** one: **make Survey-of-Heaven discovery an *event*** — per-constellation melodies, per-star tones, hover-replay, in-the-moment unlock cues (supersedes the old silent-by-default lean). New shared convention recorded in DESIGNING.md: **one `ws:pref:muted` key mutes the whole estate.**

> **▶ FEATS OF LIGHT — earned achievements on the Hall pieces, wired into THREE surfaces + a hidden capstone (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed.** Brandon, after the Hall shipped: *"move some to the Undercroft? or at least add earned badges — setting the perfect rainbow, centering the spyglass, solving the mirror maze… and put hooks into the Survey of Heaven too."* My call: **don't hide the public pieces** (the Hall is a complete public wing); instead make each bench a little **challenge with a goal**, and let mastery write to the metagame.
> - **The 9 feats** — each Hall piece now fires a raise-only `ws:flag:earned-<flag>` + a gilt "✦ Feat of Light earned" toast + a persistent topbar **goal chip**, ONLY on a genuine skill (edge-triggered, never trivially true, verified by negative tests): **Rainbow** land the impact-param on the Descartes min-deviation ray (b≈0.861, ~42°) · **Halo** the 60°-prism min-deviation ray (the 22° halo) · **Spyglass** bring M into the marked 8–12× focus band · **Camera Obscura** set the pinhole within 3% of d_opt · **Spectroscope** identify a line/source · **Polariser** rotate to full extinction · **Anamorphosis** align the reconstruction · **Iridescence** match a target colour · **Mirror Maze** a genuine PLAYER solve (not "show solution"). Flags: `earned-{rainbow,halo,spyglass,camera,spectroscope,polariser,anamorphosis,iridescence,maze}`.
> - **Surface 1 — the Hall ribbon:** `hall-of-mirrors/index.html` gained a "✦ Feats of Light — N/9 earned" ribbon (reads the flags; the on-ramp, per the hidden-features-need-an-on-ramp lesson) + a 9/9 hint that nudges toward the Undercroft without handing over the map.
> - **Surface 2 — the Survey of Heaven** (Brandon's ask): a new **feats-driven constellation "The Optician"** ("Bends every ray to its purpose.") — 9 stars in the west grounds by the Hall that kindle star-by-star as feats are earned and complete at 9/9 (brass line + engraved name + its own sub-tally + `ws:flag:sky-feats-named`). Done by extending `Sky.state` with an optional `feats` group + `visitedFromStore` mapping `ws:flag:earned-<X>`→`feat-<X>`, reusing the SAME asterism machinery as the 6 wings but **deliberately excluded from `allComplete`** so the original 6-wing capstone is structurally protected (verified both directions). Sky self-test **22/22 → 41/41**; 0 label collisions in the 9/9 state.
> - **Surface 3 — the hidden capstone (secret #12): "The Light Mixer"** (`undercroft/light-mixer.html`) — additive colour **synthesis**, the Spectroscope's exact inverse: a spinning **Newton's disc** that fuses to the area-weighted **linear-light** average (full spectrum → near-white/D65) + three **additive spotlights** (R+G=Y, G+B=C, B+R=M, R+G+B=white). Self-test **7/7** (sRGB⇄linear, disc==closed-form average, additive laws exact, radiometric not gamma-space). Unlocked by **all 9 feats** — predicate added to `tools/ws/ws.js` `WS.SECRETS` + a display row (riddle + 9 signs) in `undercroft/index.src.html`.
> - **Orchestration (ultracode):** Phase 1 = a **Workflow** (`hall-feats-of-light`) fanning out 9 worktree-isolated agents, each instrumenting one piece (shared earnFeat helper + the skill condition + goal chip) and **browser-verifying the feat actually FIRES** (drove the UI to the satisfying state, saw the toast + confirmed the localStorage flag) — staged to `/tmp/feats-of-light/`, lead copied onto main (purely additive, 531 ins). Phase 2 = 2 parallel deputies (Survey hook + the capstone piece) + lead wiring (the ribbon; `WS.SECRETS`; `forge --all` — editing the shared `ws.js` restales all 87 inlined pages). A final **end-to-end QA deputy** validated the whole chain on a **served origin** (localStorage is origin-keyed): locked baseline (capstone hidden) → 3 feats (3 stars kindle) → 9 feats (ribbon 9/9 + hint, "The Optician" complete, **The Light Mixer unlocked & loads 7/7**), original capstone unaffected, 0 overlaps, every piece still green. **PASS, no defects.**
> - **Lessons:** (1) the **shared agent-browser daemon tab gets hijacked** under heavy parallelism even with unique session names — the agents that succeeded used an **isolated headless Chrome via a private `--cdp`/CDP tab**; give heavy-parallel browser agents that isolation up front. (2) the first Workflow script failed to parse twice — **markdown ``` fences and over-escaped `\\'` inside a template-literal prompt**; build agent prompts as a `[...].join('\n')` array of plain strings, no backtick fences. (3) editing the inlined `tools/ws/ws.js` means `forge --all`, not a single-file forge.
