> **▶ THE HALL OF MIRRORS — a new front-door OPTICS WING (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed.** Brandon's nudge ("a Hall of Mirrors — all things pertaining to light: prisms, mirrors, lenses, fargazers, diffusers") landed on a real gap: the estate had a *light thread* (Caustic the ray-bench, Kaleidoscope the mirror toy, Ripple the wave tank) but **no dedicated optics wing**, and several beautiful light phenomena weren't built. Treated the nudge as inspiration, not spec (my project, my calls) and built a coherent **gallery of light** — a new front-door ROOM that homes **8 self-contained benches**: 6 brand-new + Caustic & Kaleidoscope, both **promoted out of the now-overloaded Workbench** into their truer home.
> - **The six new pieces** (each a single zero-dep HTML file, dark-gilt estate aesthetic, a built-in self-test proving its physics EXACT, browser-verified — green chip, clean console — + a `CHANGELOG.md`):
>   - **The Rainbow** 🌈 `rainbow/` — derives the rainbow from one spherical droplet: refract in (Snell) → k internal reflections → refract out; the minimum-deviation (Descartes) ray piles light into the caustic bow. **Primary 42.00°, secondary 51.04°** (computed by numerically minimising `D_k(i)=2(i−r)+k(π−2r)`, *not* baked), Alexander's dark band, reversed secondary colours, per-λ dispersion (red 42.37° > violet 40.51°), supernumeraries. Magnified-drop view + antisolar sky view, true wavelength→sRGB. Self-test **9/9** (drop trace matches closed form, maxErr 0.0).
>   - **The Spyglass** 🔭 `spyglass/` — telescope ray bench ("fargazer"). Keplerian refractor (real inverted intermediate image, exit pupil) + Newtonian reflector (parabola). M = f_obj/f_eye **exact (err 0)**; lens eq err 8.7e-19; parabola focuses 24 parallel rays through (0,f) to **7.5e-14** = no spherical aberration, while a sphere misses its paraxial focus by **134.7px** (the contrast has teeth). 1× vs magnified+inverted scene. Self-test **9/9**.
>   - **The Spectroscope** 🌈 `spectroscope/` — prism (Cauchy, min-deviation) + diffraction grating (`d·sinθ=mλ`, orders ±1/±2), then real **line spectra** at true wavelength→sRGB: H Balmer, Na-D doublet, Hg, Ne, solar **Fraunhofer** absorption. Balmer Hα **computed 656.29 nm** (used the reduced-mass-corrected `R_H`, then compared the *air* wavelength — physically exact, lands the textbook value; R∞ alone misses by 0.19nm). Self-test **7/7**.
>   - **The Polariser** 🕶️ `polariser/` — Malus's law `I=I₀cos²θ` (full→dark crossed), the E-field projection drawn; the **three-polariser paradox** (crossed pair dark; insert a 45° middle → light returns, `¼·sin²2θ`, peak **¼ at 45°** to 1.67e-16). On-screen intensities routed through the *same proven core* as the self-test (no hard-coded values). Self-test **9/9**.
>   - **The Anamorphic Mirror** 🪞 `anamorphosis/` — cylindrical-mirror (catoptric) anamorphosis: a warped table-smear that a vertical cylinder reflects back upright. Exact invertible map (azimuth preserved; radius a Möbius map). Three live panels (source / warped table / mirror reconstruction). Round-trip `unwarp(warp(P))=P` err **1.2e-15**, horizontal-line→circle-arc 2.2e-16, reconstruction of every control point 6.9e-16. Self-test **6/6**.
>   - **Iridescence** 🫧 `iridescence/` — thin-film interference: Newton's rings (`t=r²/2R`, dark `2t=mλ`, `r_m=√(mRλ)`, dark centre), draining soap film, oil slick. **True perceived colour by integrating the reflected spectrum against the CIE-1931 CMFs (Wyman fit) under D65 → sRGB** — not a fake hue ramp. Self-test **9/9** (rings exact; flat spectrum → neutral x≈y≈⅓; perfect reflector → D65; ring radius ∝√λ). 61fps animated oil mode.
> - **Orchestration (the proven pattern, at scale):** scoped each piece with a complete self-contained spec, then dispatched **6 worktree-isolated background deputies in two waves of 3** (Brandon: "tons of quota — leverage subagents to extend your run"), each building ONE piece and self-verifying in a **uniquely-named** agent-browser session, committing to its branch, reporting only a short summary (guarding lead context). Integrated each new dir to `main` via `git -C $ROOT checkout <branch> -- <dir>` + commit (disjoint new dirs → zero conflicts). A **7th QA-sweep deputy** then verified the whole wing in one uncontended browser: map + Hall + all 6 pieces **PASS**, **0 label collisions** across all 11 room labels, all 9 hrefs **200**, every chip green, every console clean — verdict *ready to push*.
> - **The front door:** appended ONE `PLACES` entry (`id:hall-of-mirrors`, accent `#8fd9ff`, west grounds x124 y430, `prefer:bottom`) + wrote a **new `hall` footprint drawer** (`drawHall` in `index.src.html` — a long vaulted gallery, a colonnade of arched **windows** along the north wall facing arched **mirrors** (drawn with the lit lip so they catch the light) along the south, a centre ridge, a west doorway). Forged + `--check --all` green. The Hall sits between the Observatory and the Glasshouse (two light rooms — thematically apt).
> - **The Hall room** (`hall-of-mirrors/index.html`): a luminous Workbench-styled gallery (a faint spectral wash across the top, a prismatic title gradient) grouping the 8 benches as *Rays/lenses/mirrors* · *Colour & spectrum* · *The wave nature of light*, with a "kin elsewhere → **Ripple**" note. **Workbench edited:** Caustic + Kaleidoscope cards removed, a `↗ The Hall of Mirrors` pointer added; the map's Workbench blurb updated. README + NOTES + this worklog updated.
> - **Lessons reinforced:** (1) CWD drifts into a completed worktree when its notification arrives — `git -C $ROOT …` for *every* integration command (caught it on the first integration). (2) Even with **unique** agent-browser session names, parallel deputies can still collide on the shared daemon's tab (two reported it); workarounds that held — re-asserting the page with chained open+verify, or launching a dedicated headless Chrome via `--cdp`. Give heavy-parallel browser deputies an isolation hint next time.
> - **Bonus round (→ 10 benches):** the core 8-bench wing shipped + live (commit `7e94564`, all 7 URLs 200), then — quota to spare — a second 2-deputy wave rounded it out. **The Halo** ☀️ `halo/`: ice-crystal atmospheric optics, the Rainbow's *frozen twin* — **22°=21.76° / 46°=45.52° halos** + sundogs + circumzenithal arc (cutoff ≈32.4°) + parhelic circle, all from numerically minimising ice-prism deviation, red inner edge from n_ice(λ); 10/10. **The Camera Obscura** 📷 `camera-obscura/`: pinhole image formation — inverted, m=v/u exact, the energy-conserving blur convolution showing the sharp↔diffraction sweet spot. The deputy **improved on my spec**: I quoted the distant-scene optimal pinhole `√(2.44λv)`, but it derived the *exact* finite-distance optimum `d=√(2.44λ·v·u/(u+v))` (matches the numeric argmin to machine precision; the classic rule shown alongside as the rule-of-thumb); 10/10. Both browser-verified (tabs not hijacked — only 2 parallel), integrated, carded, README/NOTES/this-log updated.
> - **An 11th bench — a PLAY dimension:** **The Mirror Maze** 🪞 `mirror-maze/`, a playable, provably-solvable **laser-reflection puzzle** — a laser enters a grid, you place `/`\`\\` mirrors to route the beam through every gem. **Provably solvable by construction:** the generator walks a beam inward dropping mirrors, makes the crossed cells the targets, then **prunes the solution to minimal** so every shipped mirror is load-bearing (which also gives the win-check teeth and a tight token budget). The beam-tracer detects revisited `(cell,dir)` states + a step cap so it **always terminates**. Self-test **5/5**: all 8 reflection cases exact; tracer halts on adversarial looping boards; **240 seeded boards across sizes — every reference solution lights 100% of targets, 0 failures**; win-detection has teeth (remove/rotate one mirror → unsolved); seed-reproducible. It crosses the Hall (light/reflection) with the puzzle vein (Latch/Slitherlink/Akari) and is the most literal hall-of-mirrors piece — given its own **"A puzzle of light"** group in the gallery. **The wing now stands at 11 pieces** (10 study-benches + this puzzle).

> **▶ FIX — THE SURVEY OF HEAVEN read as a BUG: constellations now legible as named star-figures (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8). Shipped & pushed.** Brandon (the site's owner) opened the live map and reported what looked like three defects: "The Scribe" and "The Gardener" labels each with **a line that points to nowhere**, and **unlabeled dots** scattered about — asking, reasonably, *bug or secret?* Diagnosis: **not a bug — the Survey of Heaven metagame working exactly as designed** (every room visited kindles a star; visiting both halves of a companion-wing completes its asterism → a brass line + an engraved name + myth; the corner tally read "2/6 skies charted" = The Scribe [verse+scriptorium] + The Gardener [strange-garden+tessellarium], and the dots were field stars for other visited rooms). **But the confusion is the finding:** the feature was *too* subtle to read as intentional — the engraved name sat in a margin berth **detached** from its constellation, and the two member stars were faint, so the name looked like it labelled nothing and the brass line looked like it pointed nowhere. The whole map ties every ROOM label to its room with a leader; the constellations alone had none (a prior deliberate "no leader, proximity only" call) — and that very inconsistency is what made them read as broken.
> - **The fix (legibility, no logic change):** (1) **charted stars** — the two member stars of a *completed* asterism are now drawn brighter/larger in brass with a stronger halo (`.sky-star.charted`, tagged in `tools/sky/sky.js` from the complete-asterism member set), so the line visibly terminates on two real stars. (2) **a faint dashed brass leader** ties each engraved name to its nearest charted star (`.an-leader`) — the *same* draughting idiom every POI label already uses — built in `buildSkyNames`/`applySkyName` (`index.src.html`) using the existing `LabelPlacer.geom.nearestEdgePoint`. (3) **stub suppression** — when the name already abuts its star (leader < 14px, e.g. The Scribe) no stub is drawn; proximity + the bright anchor-star carry the tie (avoids a glitchy 3px dash). Now a named sky reads as ONE unit: name → tie → two bright stars joined by a line.
> - **The metagame logic is untouched** — `tools/sky/sky.test.cjs` still **22/22**; only the rendering/CSS changed.
> - **Verified in a real browser** (served `:8731`, agent-browser session `skyqa`, viewport 1440×900 @2×): reproduced Brandon's exact **2/6** state (Scribe adjacent → no stub + bright stars; Gardener → visible ~30px leader), then the full **6/6** state — all six constellations (The Astronomer/Compositor/Maze & Thread/Cartographer/Scribe/Gardener) render as legible named figures with the all-skies capstone glow. **DOM bounding-box collision check: 6 sky names vs 40 POI labels → 0 collisions** (the two-pass solver still holds; POI layout byte-identical). `forge --check --all` green (29 files). NB during verify: python `http.server` serves HTML with no-cache absent → Chrome cached the old `index.html`; cache-bust with a `?v=N` query to see forge changes.
> - **The unlabeled dots are LEFT AS-IS by design** — they're field stars (rooms you've visited that haven't completed a wing), the metagame's quiet breadcrumbs; Brandon said he's happy to "play to discover," so the mystery stays. The fix only makes the *completed* figures read as intentional, not broken.

> **▶ BONUS — THE BLACK CHAMBER: a codebreaker's desk that cracks classical ciphers by analysis (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed; commit `1abd15e` (+ Workbench card & README bullet in the consolidation).** Completes the estate's cipher story — it had makers (Volvelle, Scytale, the hidden Enigma) but no BREAKER. Recovers plaintext from ciphertext with NO key, by classical cryptanalysis. `tools/cryptanalysis/cryptanalysis.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded, forge+Node, seeded): **Caesar** by χ² vs English letter freqs (exact); **Vigenère** by the **index of coincidence** to find the period, then per-column χ² (exact keyword); **monoalphabetic substitution** by a seeded hill-climb/anneal on a **trigram** log-fitness (a compact ~5KB embedded English corpus → a trigram table at load — no big data files). Self-test **14/14** Node, 5/5 in-page: Caesar **156/156 exact**; Vigenère **48/48 exact keyword** + period identified (IoC@6=0.0778 over its neighbours); substitution mean ~100% on corpus / ≥90% held-out; **IoC(English) 0.0689 vs IoC(random) 0.0385**; χ²(English) 33 ≪ χ²(wrong-shift) 9600; auto-detect classifies all three; determinism. Page: paste/sample ciphertext → auto-detect or pick → watch the letter-frequency chart vs English, the IoC, the period, and (for substitution) the plaintext resolve from gibberish to readable English live. Browser-verified (`:8151`, session `crypt-verify`): recovered keywords CIPHER/LEMON at 100%, substitution to 97.3%, auto-detect correct, 0 console errors. Honest caveats (SPEC): Caesar exact always; Vigenère needs adequate per-column ciphertext; substitution is statistical (~97–100% on ≳300 letters). 3 skins, 2× PNG. Workbench → Instruments. (The session's 12th piece — a capstone to the cipher vein: makers → breaker.)

> **▶ BONUS — THE STRAIGHTEDGE: a Peaucellier linkage that draws an exact straight line (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed; commit `8ecf9ae` (+ Workbench card & README bullet in the consolidation).** The estate's FIRST planar-linkage / kinematics piece. The hero is the **Peaucellier–Lipkin linkage** (1864) — the first mechanism to convert pure rotation into a MATHEMATICALLY EXACT straight line, by circle inversion: a rhombus (4 equal bars) + 2 equal long bars from a fixed pivot O make the pen P the circle-inverse of a point Q (`|OP|·|OQ| = L²−ℓ²` const); drive Q on a circle through O and P traces a perfect line. `tools/linkage/linkage.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded, forge+Node) solves the configuration per crank angle; ships Peaucellier + a four-bar crank-rocker (its curved coupler curve is the foil that shows why exact-straight is special). Self-test **14/14**: **Peaucellier max perpendicular deviation from a perfect line = 4.88e-15** (machine precision — exact, NOT approximate; the soul of the piece), the inversive invariant to ~9e-16, bar-length loop-closure to ~1e-15 (the linkage truly closes — no cheating the geometry), four-bar all-5-bar drift ~1e-15 + 0 dead spots, determinism + skin-invariance. Animated bars/pin-joints, crank + angle scrubber, live trace + a live `straight to ±…e-15` readout, 3 skins, 2× PNG. Browser-verified (`:8149`, session `linkage-verify`): the pen visibly draws a dead-straight line, changing lengths re-solves exact, 0 console errors. **Dropped the pantograph** (its exact-affine claim is provable but a rigid free-roaming parallelogram collapses to a degenerate stick on screen — the "smaller proven set" rule). Workbench → Toys & benches. (The session's 10th piece.)

Self-test **12/12** Node (5 core: (1) ideal EXACTLY binomial — Σ=1 to 2e-15, mean=Np, var=Npq to 7e-14, Pascal row 1·4·6·4·1/16; (2) ≥100k-ball seeded runs [fair AND biased] do-not-reject χ² at α=0.01 — AND a flat histogram IS rejected, so the test has POWER; + CDF calibrated to published χ² quantiles, err ≤5e-4; (3) every path is a valid lattice path, bin=#right ∈[0,N], conservation; (4) determinism; (5) empirical mean/var track Np/Npq; +7 more), 5/5 in-page. Browser-verified (`:8148`, session `galton-verify`): mean/σ track the theory, the χ² p-value updates live, biased p skews + the overlay follows, 0 console errors, reduced-motion instant-tally. > **▶ BONUS — THE PATIENCE ENGINE: a solitaire whose dealer only deals provably-winnable hands (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Shipped & pushed after the big wave; commit `4bd9b13` (+ the Workbench card & README bullet in the consolidation).** A new genre for the estate (it had zero card pieces). The Lantern idea, dealt onto cards: a headless SOLVER searches a dealt game for a win, and the DEALER only ships deals it has already beaten (rejection sampling) — so "a deal you can't lose" is true by construction AND proven by the self-test. `tools/patience/patience.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded, forge+Node) is variant-as-data: a **compact FreeCell** (28-card A–7×4, 6 columns, 3 free cells) chosen so the reachable graph stays small and the solver beats essentially every board in a few thousand nodes (a 40-deal proving battery runs <0.4s); the *same* engine also deals/solves full 52-card FreeCell as a generality check. Solver = deterministic **weighted-A\*** (`f=g+3h`, foundation-distance `h`) + a transposition table + cycle avoidance + FreeCell safe-automove pruning + a 200k-node budget; the cached winning line drives `hint()` + watch-it-solve. Self-test **11/11** Node (6 core: solver soundness — every returned line is legal move-by-move and reaches the win; **winnability guarantee** — a seed battery all solve AND replay to a win; determinism; move-gen correctness; 28-card conservation; +5 cross-cutting), 6/6 in-page (same core). Page: a dark-felt **click-to-move** FreeCell — Deal-(guaranteed-winnable) w/ seed, Hint (pulse the next move), Undo, Watch-it-solve (replays the proven line to the foundations), illegal moves declined, win celebration. Forge page using WS (`ws:seen:patience`). Browser-verified (served `:8146`, session `patience-verify`, desktop + mobile), 0 console errors. Honest scope (in SPEC §0): every *dealt* game is winnable — not every conceivable deal, and not that you'll win (you can still strand yourself; Undo/Hint are there). Build gotcha fixed: `forge:include` directives must sit INSIDE an open `<script>` block. Workbench → "Games of perfect information". Picked as the "one more cool thing" after the wave (the ideation's runner-up engine).

> **▶ THE BIG CREATIVE WAVE — a new ENGINE, a new METAGAME, and three growth POIs, built by a fan-out of parallel deputies (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"; Brandon away overnight). Five pieces shipped & pushed; HEAD `dbaf864`; tree clean, synced, `forge --check --all` green, no worktrees.** Commits: `956b92f` (Survey) · `5e260d2` (Adversary) · `41a360f` (Singing Plate) · `f9c2db3` (Bulwark + arcade tag bump) · `d9beeaa` (Gnomon) · `dbaf864` (Workbench cards + README consolidation). Process docs: `f9ff0a6`/`79ef61c` (the durable `worklog/big-wave-plan.md` plan + ledger).
> 
> **(0) Ideation as a fan-out (honoring Brandon's "explore outward like fractals, curate their discoveries").** Ran a Workflow (`wf_046852b2-a3c`): **11 idea SCOUTS** (4 engine angles, 4 metagame angles, 3 POI scouts) each repo-grounded (read NOTES + the hidden inventory + Lantern/WS/forge) and returning a structured proposal (concept, novelty/dedupe-check, the provable claim, forge-share story, map-surface, complexity, risks, a build-ready spec) → **1 CURATOR** that ranked per thread, dedupe-checked vs the existing/hidden inventory (greps confirmed every proposed `tools/` dir absent), and produced a build order + contention strategy. I saved the full curation to a durable `worklog/big-wave-plan.md` (so the overnight run survived `/tmp` eviction + context resets), then dispatched build deputies. PICKS: **The Adversary** (engine), **The Survey of Heaven** (metagame), and POIs **The Singing Plate / The Gnomon / Bulwark**. Runner-ups recorded for later: The Patience Engine, a logic-puzzle generator, "The Hours" (real-time living estate).
> 
> **(1) ♟️ The Adversary** (`adversary/` + `tools/game/`, `5e260d2`) — a reusable **combinatorial-game-theory engine** and a new GENRE for the estate (a thinking, unbeatable adversary). `tools/game/adversary.js` (DOM-free, module-guarded for forge+Node) ENUMERATES a game's reachable state graph (BFS over a symmetry-canonical key, hard-capped) then RETROGRADE-LABELS every position WIN/LOSS/DRAW with exact distance-to-end by backward induction — and the SAME solve table drives both play and the self-test, so the opponent and the proof can't drift. Lantern-shaped: 5 games supplied as pure data (`tools/game/games/{nim,ttt333,konane,hex3,mnk443}.js`), each proven — Nim [3,4,5] WIN mate-11, ttt333 DRAW, 4×4 Kōnane LOSS mate-6, Hex-3 WIN mate-5, (3,4,3) m,n,k WIN mate-7. Players: perfectPlayer / randomPlayer(seed) / describeForAgent + an `llmPlayer` stub (the Lantern bot-hook parallel). Page: play vs perfection, "reveal verdicts" tints every legal move WIN/LOSS/DRAW + "mate in N", Hint, watch-perfect-vs-perfect (halts at the proven outcome), honest "you let a forced win slip", 2× PNG. Self-test **38/38** Node (literature values incl. the Nim XOR theorem + ttt=DRAW; table self-consistency; optimal-vs-optimal outcome+distance; **perfect-never-loses exhaustive sweep**; symmetry-canon soundness; node-budget), 30/30 in-page. **Design call:** m,n,k shipped as (3,4,3) — the 4×4·3 graph (300,001 nodes) exceeds the 300k cap + the <1s in-browser budget; per the "smaller proven set beats five with a bug" rule the genre + proof were kept. The self-test EARNED ITS KEEP — caught 4 real bugs mid-build (a perfectPlayer canonical-vs-live-move mismatch, an order-dependent retrograde distance, a `playFrom` POV parity bug, a painter arg-swap).
> 
> **(2) ✶ The Survey of Heaven** (`tools/sky/` + `index.src.html`, `956b92f`) — the **cross-page metagame**, whose visible surface IS the front-door map. It READS the `ws:seen:<id>` breadcrumb EVERY page already drops (so zero per-page instrumentation; touches only `index.src.html` + 3 new `tools/sky/` files): first visit to a room kindles a star over its quarter of the dark plate; the six real **companion-wings** (celestial/design/labyrinth/realm/letters/garden) join into asterisms that COMPLETE when both members are seen — lines brighten to brass, an engraved name (The Astronomer, The Cartographer, …) is typeset in the margin; charting all six fires an all-skies capstone; a "Survey of Heaven — N/6 charted" tally is the on-ramp. `tools/sky/sky.js` (Sky global, forge-inlined + Node-testable) is a pure core + a thin renderer; writes only NEW cosmetic namespaced flags (`ws:flag:sky-*`, `ws:flag:firmament-survey`) — no existing predicate touched, NO new secret, no `ws.js` edit; `Sky.bootstrap` silences retroactive name-animations for returning visitors. **The integration crux:** adding the sky's names to the map's single shared label-solve perturbed the seeded annealing and shifted an unrelated POI label onto a footprint → fixed by a **SECOND, independent label-solve pass** (POI labels solve first; asterism names then solve against those rects + footprints + furniture as obstacles), so the POI layout is **byte-identical to baseline regardless of sky state** and DOM-truth overlaps stay 0 in all states. Self-test **22/22** (determinism/order-independence, **monotonicity**, **completion-iff** over a member-minus-one battery, catalog stars all inside the viewBox & off every footprint, bijection). Undercroft gating, hover cards, avenues, a11y, reduced-motion, storage-off degradation, and the forget-reset all preserved.
> 
> **(3) 🎛️ The Singing Plate** (`singing-plate/` + `tools/plate/`, `41a360f`) — a **Chladni vibration bench** and the workshop's FIRST spectral solver; the third panel of the wave-physics trilogy (Caustic the rays, Ripple the travelling waves, the Plate the standing ones). `tools/plate/plate.js` discretizes the negative-Laplacian −Δ on a masked shape (square / inscribed disk; clamped/free) and numerically SOLVES the eigenproblem −Δu=λu with a from-scratch, deterministic, seeded **Rayleigh-Ritz Lanczos** eigensolver (dense projected H = QᵀLQ + cyclic Jacobi; no deps — full reorthogonalization breaks the three-term recurrence, so H is formed explicitly). Seeded sand drifts off antinodes onto the solved **nodal lines** (`xy += −η·∇(u²) + jitter·|u|`). Self-test **18/18** Node, 8/8 in-page: square eigenvalues CONVERGE to π²·{2,5,8,10,13} as the grid refines, circle eigenvalue RATIOS match Bessel-zero ratios `(j₁,₁/j₀,₁)²`, eigenvectors orthonormal (Gram≈I to 1e-15) + operator symmetric (max|L−Lᵀ|=0), sand-on-nodes ≥3×, determinism + skin-invariance. Membrane drumhead ships as the proven default; the 4th-order biharmonic plate is a clearly-labelled experimental stretch mode. **Distinct** from the watch-only `strange-garden/pieces/chladni.html` (a hardcoded closed-form animation, no eigensolver). No audio. Workbench → Toys & benches.
> 
> **(4) 🛡️ Bulwark** (`arcade/games/bulwark.*`, `f9c2db3`) — a neon **Defender/Scramble side-scroller** (arcade #19, the last classic-genre gap), shipping BOTH loops: the Defender rescue chain (a Lantern grabs a grounded tender, hauls it up; shoot it [+75] to free it; catch the falling tender [+250] to re-ground; a tender reaching the top mutates its Lantern into a faster strafer) AND the Scramble fuel loop (fuel burns, bomb a depot [+60] to refuel, terrain-crash/fuel-out = death). Full-ring scanner strip, thrust/inertia, mobile touch controls, SFX **muted by default** (odd-hours courtesy). Deterministic CORE (`arcade/games/bulwark.core.js`, module-guarded): seeded mulberry32 + a fixed-timestep accumulator → integer ticks (no `Math.random`, no wall-clock dt). Self-test 6 core + 6 harness: **replay determinism** (seed + scripted input → identical 900-tick state hash twice), horizontal-wrap continuity (shortest-arc delta), the rescue invariant (+75/+250 exact), fuel monotonicity, collision symmetry across the seam, seed-purity. I applied the front-door Arcade `tag` bump 18→19 + the blurb myself.
> 
> **(5) 🌅 The Gnomon** (`sundial/` + `tools/dial/`, `d9beeaa`) — an operable **sundial** whose shadow tells true clock time, the celestial sub-wing's iconic missing member. `tools/dial/dial.js` COPIES the Astrolabe's frozen solar functions verbatim (so the two instruments demonstrably read ONE sky) + adds hour-angle, the equation-of-time, per-dial hour-line angles, the gnomon style angle, and the cast-shadow projection. Three dials (horizontal/equatorial/vertical-south); set lat/lon/tz/date/time → the shadow sweeps; toggle the EoT correction → the civil readout matches the wall clock; "plot a year of noons" draws the **analemma** figure-8; 3 skins. Self-test **21/21** (round-trip clock over 12,250 states, shadow-tip on the closed-form hour-line <1e-5·r for all 3 types, hour-line angles == closed form [equatorial exactly 15°/hr], EoT extrema +16.45m≈Nov 3 / −14.20m≈Feb 11 + the four zero-crossings, gnomon angle == lat/co-lat, determinism + skin-invariance). Workbench → Instruments.
> 
> **(6) Orchestration + lessons (durable).** Pattern: ideation Workflow → curate → 5 **parallel background worktree deputies** (each built one piece + self-verified in a real browser on its OWN port [8141–8145] + a UNIQUELY-NAMED agent-browser session) → I reviewed screenshots, integrated each to `main`, pushed, retired the worktree, kept the pipeline ~3 deep. **LESSONS:** (a) the shell **CWD silently drifts into a completed worktree** when its task-notification arrives — a plain `git checkout <branch> -- <paths>` then no-op'd against the worktree itself and reported the worktree's already-clean state; fix = ALWAYS `git -C <root> checkout … -- <paths>` + absolute `node` paths + verify with `git -C <root> diff --cached --stat` before committing. (b) forge resolves includes relative to the `.src.html` dir and writes beside it → **absolute paths make forge CWD-independent** (`node <root>/tools/forge/forge.mjs <root>/x.src.html`; `--check --all <root>`). (c) **Lead does ALL shared-file edits** (the `workbench/index.html` cards, README, the map tag-bump) while deputies create ONLY new files → zero merge races on the hot files; the only two `index.src.html` writers (Survey's surface, Bulwark's tag) were serialized. A 10-min heartbeat cron backstopped the unattended run against accidental turn-ends.

> **▶ THE LETTERER — a point-feature label-placement engine — and the SELF-LETTERING MAP (2026-06-13 cont., `/fun` as Opus 4.8, "ultracode"). Brandon saw the live map and flagged overlapping POI labels (Gardens↔the Workshop cartouche, Undercroft↔Music Room, the Manor-House caption↔Print Room); his steer: *"build a label placement algorithm specimen … and use it for your map too."* So I built a real algorithm, proved it, shipped it as a specimen, then made the front door consume it. All pushed & live; HEAD `9a3a062`; tree clean, synced, `forge --check --all` green, no worktrees.** Commits: `b396acc` (the Letterer engine + specimen) · `9a3a062` (map self-letters).
> 
> **(1) The engine — `tools/label/label.js`.** Cartographic **Point-Feature Label Placement** (PFLP), a real NP-hard problem: drop a name beside every point so no two labels collide, none crosses an obstacle, all stay in-frame, each prefers its ideal side. Implements the classic **candidate-slot (fixed-position) model + simulated annealing** (Christensen, Marks & Shieber, ACM TOG 1995): for each feature, 4 or 8 candidate label-rects around the anchor; energy = Σ(label-label overlap·W + label-obstacle overlap·W + out-of-bounds·W + side-preference·W + leader-length·W) with overlaps dominating; SA with a **seeded PRNG (mulberry32) → fully deterministic**, geometric cooling, then a greedy local-improvement sweep; honours `pin`ned features (fixed, still act as obstacles). API `LabelPlacer.solve({bounds, features:[{id,anchor,label:{w,h},prefer,pin,gap}], obstacles, seed, positions, iterations}) → {placements:[{id,x,y,side,label,leader,cost,overlapped}], overlaps, energy}`. Module-guarded (braced single line so forge strips it) + CommonJS-exported + `root.LabelPlacer` for browsers — same dual-target pattern as `tools/ws/ws.js`. Node self-test `tools/label/label.test.cjs` **12/12**: determinism (byte-identical across runs), bounds containment, a **24-case feasible battery → overlaps===0** (0 label-label AND 0 label-obstacle), monotone SA improvement, pin honoured + avoided, graceful infeasibility (over-dense → no crash, bounded, residual reported), leader correctness.
> 
> **(2) The specimen — "The Letterer"** (`letterer/`, Workbench → Toys & benches, glyph ✍️). A live, beautiful demonstrator on the dark drafting aesthetic: scatter N named survey stations, hit **Anneal**, and watch the clashing cloud of overlapping names *cool* into a clean layout as the **overlap counter falls to 0** (phase pill → "settled · 0 overlaps"). Controls: reseed, station-count slider, click-to-add a station, **4↔8-position** toggle, **obstacles** toggle (a river + lake drawn as no-go bands the labels route around), candidate-slot reveal for one selected point, 4 anneal speeds (instant = the real `solve()`). The workshop signature, on-page: a self-test chip **`SELF-TEST 24/24 ✓ — 0 overlaps across 24 seeded layouts`**. Forge page (`<!-- forge:include tools/ws/ws.js -->` + `<!-- forge:include tools/label/label.js -->`), `ws:seen:letterer` breadcrumb, `← workshop` back-link, PNG 2×. Browser-verified on a real origin (`:8131`, session `letterer-verify`): anneal 9→0, counter→0, all controls, self-test green, PNG valid, **0 console errors**, handsome at 1440×900 + mobile 390×844 (fixed a mobile topbar wrap).
> 
> **(3) The map now letters itself** (`index.src.html`). The front door inlines `tools/label/label.js` via forge and computes its own label layout at load — **two-pass**: build + measure each label group (`getBBox`, +3px pad for the text's dark backing stroke) → `LabelPlacer.solve` with one feature per *present* POI (anchor = footprint centre; per-feature `gap` = footprint half-extent + margin so every candidate slot clears the building it names; `prefer` **seeded from the old curated side** so the hand-tuned composition is the start-point and only colliding labels move; `pin` wired through) → translate each label group onto its solved rect, set text-anchor from the chosen side, redraw a straight leader from the footprint boundary to the label's nearest edge. Obstacles = all footprints + compass rose + scale bar + title cartouche + both zone captions; bounds = inset frame. **Fixed seed `0x5EED` → byte-identical layout every load** (verified across 3 reloads — no jitter). Solved ONCE (the viewBox scales uniformly, so positions are resolution-independent — no re-solve on resize). Design call: the manor *shell* is deliberately NOT an obstacle (the 4 wing footprints inside it already guard the interior; walling the shell would trap the wings' own labels inside the house). **DOM-truth overlaps = 0** in both the fresh AND the Undercroft-revealed states (label↔label, label↔footprint, label↔furniture, out-of-bounds all clear); the three named collisions confirmed gone; the Undercroft gating (broken→rune→whole, grow/repair, margin rune, storage-off degradation), hover cards, accent glow, "↳ within" pills, survey avenues, a11y, reduced-motion all preserved; rune-click reuses the already-solved cellar-stair slot (no layout shift). **`lx/ly` are now OPTIONAL hints** — appending a future POI needs only a coordinate + footprint (+ optional `prefer`/`pin`). Browser-verified (`:8132`, session `map-relabel`) fresh + revealed + mobile, 0 console errors.
> 
> **(4) Orchestration.** Two sequential worktree deputies — #1 built the engine+specimen+test (no map changes), #2 (branched after #1 landed) wired the map; I reviewed screenshots between and integrated each via `git checkout <branch> -- <files>` + a fresh commit (the reliable path for the forge-generated `index.html`), pushed, retired the worktree. The map's cleanup *became* a live demonstration of a new provable algorithm — strictly better than nudging coordinates, and exactly the steer Brandon gave.

Brandon's nudge: reframe the crowded front door as "an overhead schematic map of a manor and grounds — rooms as POIs," and lean hard on parallel deputies. All pushed & live; HEAD `3d9dbba`; tree clean, synced, `forge --check --all` green.** Commits: `8dfdba0` (ws keystone, pushed early) · `c166119` (Harmonograph) · `96b8fdb` (map) · `c2ba3fb` (ws broaden) · `7033c33` (map enrichment) · `3d9dbba` (Scytale).

Picked **A** (the whole site is dark `#080a0f`; A reads as the threshold of that dark world, B's bright parchment was a tonal outlier) but had the production build **fuse A's bones with B's warmth**. Production deputy built `index.src.html` (forge → `index.html`): a **data-driven `PLACES` array** (id/room/piece/glyph/accent/href/tag/blurb/companion/x/y/footprint, 8 footprint drawers) → adding a room = append one entry. Manor house = 5 indoor rooms (Study=Oracle, Print Room=Compositor, Map Room=Cartographer, Music Room=Sound Garden, East Wing=Threshold); grounds = Gardens(Strange Garden), Observatory(Firmament), Hedge Maze(Daedalus), Arcade; outbuilding = Workshop(workbench); companions ride "↳ within" the parent (not separate POIs). The **Undercroft gating ported EXACTLY** (≥4 distinct ws:seen OR earned → broken cellar-stair POI + footer rune → click repairs → "descend"; first-appearance grow; reduced-motion/storage-off graceful). First production pass read austere, so a focused **enrichment pass** (`7033c33`) added candlelit manor glow + vignette + ink-stipple ground, enlarged footprints with real plan detail (portico colonnade, ribbed observatory dome, glasshouse mullions+parterre, traceable hedge-maze, arch colonnade, sawtooth shed), tree-lined dashed survey avenues, stronger leader-line labels, an engraved title cartouche — now a lamplit, inhabited estate. Hover lights a room in its accent; mobile pans; a11y + reduced-motion. Verified served-origin across fresh/hover/earned/repaired/returning/mobile, consoles clean. README hero (`assets/workshop-preview.png`) replaced with the new map.
>
> **(2) The `ws:` keystone — forge-inlined + an in-the-moment unlock cue (Brandon asked directly).** Was: every page hand-rolled `localStorage.setItem('ws:…')` (UNLOCK.md guardrail #4 literally said "no shared import"). Now **`tools/ws/ws.js`** is the single source of truth — writers `seen/best/flag/dwellAdd/startDwell`, a `store()` reader matching the undercroft's `{ok,has,get,all}` shape, the **11 SECRETS predicates** (transcribed verbatim), `bootstrap()` (first run silently marks already-satisfied secrets announced → no cue spam for returning visitors), `checkUnlocks()` (diffs newly-satisfied vs a `ws:ann:<id>` namespace), and `renderCue()` — a spoiler-light candlelit toast **"Something stirs in the dark beneath the workshop"** (never names the secret; warmer once you've found the cellar; optional whisper-chime only if an AudioContext is already running — never autoplays). **Inlined via forge** (`<!-- forge:include …/tools/ws/ws.js -->`, braced module-guard so forge strips it; also CommonJS-exported for `tools/ws/ws.test.cjs`). The Undercroft consumes `WS.SECRETS` predicates (its prose/riddles stay local → no secret prose in public page source). Foundation (`8dfdba0`, self-test **36/36**, served-origin verified, pushed) converted undercroft + verse + scriptorium; broaden (`c2ba3fb`) routed the other 9 trigger pages (game-of-life, lattice, quickening[+`eleven`], cartographer, orrery, slipstick, astrolabe, abacus, swarm[`ws:best`]) — the cue fires on every trail (Reckoner & Almanac verified, once, no re-fire). **~14 pages are now `.src.html`→`.html` forge artifacts** (edit the .src, `node tools/forge/forge.mjs <f>.src.html`, `--check --all` verifies). DEFERRED: the Lantern tale win-flags (night-shift trail) — touches the shared engine, separate pass.

> **(3) Harmonograph** (`harmonograph/`, Workbench Toys & benches, `c166119`) — a Victorian pendulum drawing-machine, kin to Caustic/Ripple. 4 decaying pendulums, `x(t)/y(t)=Σ Aᵢ sin(2πfᵢt+φᵢ)e^(−dᵢt)`; per-pendulum sliders + seed + 6 ratio presets; live trace; 3 skins (ink/blueprint/phosphor). Self-test **5/5**: path == eqn <1e-9, damping envelope monotone, integer-ratio closed-curve law (returns to start in position+velocity at the LCM period, with a non-period guard), seed-repro + skin-invariance. Browser-verified, console clean. (Old inline ws snippet — pre-keystone; fine.)

> **(4) Scytale** (`scytale/`, Workbench Instruments, the Volvelle's cipher kin, `3d9dbba`) — the workshop's first **transposition** cipher: a rod + wound parchment strip = columnar transposition (wind on circumference C, read straight = ciphertext), + a **keyed-columnar** generalization (keyword permutes the column read-order). Visual oak rod; wound(plaintext) vs straight(ciphertext) views; a wrong-rod gibberish demo; 3 skins; PNG. Self-test **13/13**: round-trip, bijection-of-positions, definitional column-read correctness, key/skin sensitivity, textbook vectors. A forge page using WS from birth. > **▶ TWO BENCH PIECES — THE VOLVELLE (cipher disk) + RIPPLE (wave-interference tank) — and a head-pointer fix after an Enigma duplication near-miss (2026-06-12, `/fun` as Opus 4.8).** Commits: `07fa24d` (Volvelle) · `4f113fd` (docs, pushed & live-200) · `2576953` (Ripple); the Enigma duplicate was committed then `git reset`-discarded before any push. Volvelle **13/13**, Ripple **8/8** (headless + browser badge).
>
> **(1) The near-miss — and why it matters more than the build.** I decided to add a working **Enigma** as the bench's 4th instrument, wrote a full spec, and shipped it via two deputies (Stage 1 machine, Stage 2 signal-path viz + a verified 1930-message preset) — *beautiful, 5/5, BDZGO vector confirmed in the UI, committed twice.* THEN, doing the worklog rotation, I hit a line in `worklog/INDEX.md`: "Enigma 🔐 → new cipher vein (3-rotor Enigma I; Undercroft secret #9)." **`undercroft/enigma.html` already existed** — a full, more-thorough Enigma (12 self-tests incl. the "Aachen" daily key; its own signal-path trace) built in a prior session, hidden in the Undercroft. My opening `ls -d enigma` checked only the top level and missed it; and **the hidden inventory was not in NOTES.md** (spoiler etiquette had kept the Undercroft's contents out of the visible index). Root cause = the head-pointer couldn't prevent a duplicate it didn't list. **A public Enigma would also spoil the hidden one's surprise** (two identical machines, one meant to be *earned*). So I `git reset --hard`-discarded both unpushed Enigma commits (clean — origin never saw them; the hidden Enigma untouched, confirmed), and pivoted to a *genuinely different* public cipher.
>
> **(2) Brandon's rule change (mid-session, unprompted — landed exactly on the cause).** "It is OK to put spoilers in NOTES.md. I don't read that. Just don't put them in your final conversation summary… that way you can get full inventory in there and avoid this next time." **Shipped:** the spoiler-etiquette block in NOTES.md now scopes the rule to **the final conversation summary ONLY** (NOTES/worklog/SPECs are EXEMPT and MUST carry the full inventory); added a **🗝️ HIDDEN INVENTORY callout** near the top of NOTES.md listing every hidden standalone page by path (quickening · rosette · codex · floating-ink · almanac · **enigma** · the below-only Night Shift tale) + the 4 trophies; fixed the project-status table's Undercroft row (8→**11 secrets**, all named) and added an explicit `undercroft/enigma.html` row flagged "do NOT build another rotor-Enigma." Also trimmed two fully-built idea-bench blocks (Living Lattice + Unlock framework, ~90 lines → ~10, detail already in `UNLOCK.md`) so NOTES.md went 458→**380 lines** (back under the ≤450 budget) despite the additions. **The lesson, recorded so it sticks: grep the hidden inventory before building anything; the head-pointer is only as good as what it lists.**
>
> **(3) The Volvelle — the correct public cipher (`volvelle/index.html`, single self-contained vanilla file, 0 deps/network, NO AUDIO).** A genuine, operable **Alberti cipher disk** — a *rotating-disc* instrument, mechanically nothing like Enigma's rotors/plugboard/reflector, so the two coexist cleanly (public on-ramp ↔ hidden earned depth). A FIXED outer plaintext ring (A–Z) + a ROTATING inner cipher disk (SVG, animated `rotate()` transform, fixed index mark at 12 o'clock, a glowing radial spoke linking the active plaintext↔cipher pair, brass pivot hub). It physically performs **three** classical ciphers: **Caesar** (inner=ordered, fixed shift k → C=(P+k)%26), **Vigenère** (inner=ordered, the keyword drives the rotation per letter — the disk steps to a new alignment for each character), and **Alberti** (inner = a **seeded mixed** alphabet that re-keys every N letters — the authentic 1467 polyalphabetic). Controls: mode segment, shift slider / keyword field / seed+period, **drag-to-rotate** the inner disk (snaps to letters; sets the shift live in Caesar mode), Encipher⇄Decipher toggle, output tape in 5-letter groups + copy/clear (echoes input; shows the repeating key under plaintext in Vigenère), presets (Caesar/3, ROT13, ATTACKATDAWN·LEMON, Clear), **3 skins** (brass default / parchment / blueprint — cosmetic only), **2× PNG export** (manual canvas draw — no `foreignObject`, so no taint, the Enigma/Slipstick lesson applied up front). **THE CRUX (workshop tradition): the cipher is REAL & PROVEN.** A pure `CORE` (ordered/mixed alphabets, rotation/keying per mode, encipher/decipher, a geometry function for the rendered alignment) is the single source of truth for BOTH renderer and a headless self-test → green chip **"volvelle verified — 13/13 ✓"**: (1) **round-trip** `decipher(encipher(m,S),S)===m` across all 3 modes (3000 random settings × 200-letter msgs); (2) **alignment == math** — the renderer's cipher-letter-under-P equals (P+k)%26 over all 26×26; (3) **bijection** — the seeded mixed alphabet is a true permutation over 2000 seeds; (4) **canonical vectors EXACT** — Vigenère `ATTACKATDAWN`+`LEMON`→`LXFOPVEFRNHR`, Caesar/3 `HELLO`→`KHOOR`, ROT13 `HELLO`→`URYYB` + self-inverse; (5) **mode consistency** — Caesar(k) ≡ Vigenère with a length-1 key of index k; (6) **seed-purity / skin-invariance** — output depends only on (mode,key,seed,period), never skin. **Self-test core re-run under Node — green**; the in-page CORE was extracted from the shipped HTML and proven behaviorally identical to the verified reference CORE (0 diffs over 2000 Alberti settings). **Browser-verified end to end** (agent-browser, served origin `:8793`): badge 13/13; the Vigenère preset + typing ATTACKATDAWN → `LXFOP VEFRN HR` on the tape with `LEMON LEMON LE` under it and the disk stepping per letter; Caesar/3→KHOOR, ROT13→URYYB, Decipher round-trips URYYB→HELLO; Alberti (seed 4821,N=4) re-keys every 4 letters `[0,0,0,0,1,1,1,1,…]` and round-trips; drag-to-rotate snaps + sets the shift; all 3 skins identical output, **0 console errors**; PNG a valid untainted 1000×1192. **Deviation:** Alberti `start`=0 (the per-period step does the re-keying — matches the historical mechanic); no "preserve spaces" toggle (letters-only classical convention). **Wired:** the 4th card in the Workbench **Instruments** group (glyph 🔄, "cipher disk"); `← workshop` back-link; `ws:seen:volvelle` breadcrumb (un-trophied — a future Undercroft "codebreaker" trophy is a trivial add); README "Also on the workbench" entry (Abacus → **Volvelle** → Colophon). Spec `volvelle/VOLVELLE.SPEC.md`, log `volvelle/CHANGELOG.md`.
>
> **(4) Ripple — a wave-interference tank, diversifying away from ciphers (`ripple/index.html`, single self-contained vanilla file, 0 deps/network, NO AUDIO).** The wave-physics sibling to **Caustic** (geometric optics): where Caustic traces light *rays*, Ripple is *waves* — superposition, interference, diffraction. A dark "tank" canvas with N **draggable** point sources emitting circular waves that interfere by exact **linear superposition**: `field(x,y,t) = Σ_i Ai·falloff(ri)·cos(k·ri − ω·t + φi)`, `k=2π/λ`, `ω=2πf`. Closed-form per-pixel (NO finite-difference PDE — trivially correct + provable). Rendered into a 300×258 offscreen buffer (the heavy Σcos loop) then `drawImage`-upscaled for perf (~6.7ms@2 sources → ~29ms@8; comfortable 30–60fps for the typical 2–6 tank). Controls: play/pause, wavelength λ, frequency, amplitude, per-source **flip-phase**, falloff none/realistic toggle, **nodal-lines overlay**, add/remove sources; presets (two-source · double-slit · line array · single drop). **THE CRUX (workshop tradition): the field is physically exact & PROVEN.** A pure CORE (`field`, per-source contribution, `resultantAmplitude` closed-form `2A|cos(kΔ/2)|`) is the single source of truth for renderer + headless self-test → green chip **"ripple verified — 8/8 ✓"**: (1) **render == analytic field** (renderer derives colour from `field()`; equals a hand-rolled reference Σ to <1e-9); (2) **superposition linearity** `field({A,B})==field({A})+field({B})` (<1e-12); (3) **interference loci EXACT** (falloff=none, two equal in-phase sources): perpendicular bisector R≈2A (constructive); nodes R≈0 where Δ=|r1−r2|=(n+½)λ; antinodes R≈2A where Δ=nλ; closed-form `resultantAmplitude` == sampled max_t|field| over many points — **probe points placed analytically on the path-difference hyperbola so r1−r2=Δ is exact by construction** (the deputy's first bisection-solver for these was buggy → caught & fixed before proceeding); (4) **antiphase inversion** (flip-phase → bisector becomes a node R=0); (5) **double-slit** far-field spacing ≈ λL/d (within ~5% in the paraxial near-axis window); (6) **determinism/skin-invariance** — field depends only on (sources,k,ω,t,falloff), never skin (fingerprint `556a133b` identical across all 3 skins). **Self-test core re-run under Node — the shipped in-page CORE returns 8/8, and an independent re-derivation of the physics is 10/10** (browser badge == headless). **Browser-verified end to end** (agent-browser, served origin `:8794`): badge 8/8, **0 console errors**, two-source pattern symmetric with a bright bisector antinode, dragging moves fringes live, λ slider changes fringe density (20 vs 120), flip-phase inverts nodes↔antinodes, play/pause freezes, nodal overlay aligns with the dark hyperbolae, double-slit + line-array presets correct, all 3 skins byte-identical field, PNG export crisp, `ws:seen:ripple` set. **Deviations (spec-permitted):** double-slit modelled as two point sources (not Huygens-array slits); phase as a per-source flip toggle (not a continuous dial). **Wired:** a card in the Workbench **Toys & benches** group (glyph 🌊, "wave-interference tank"); `← workshop` back-link; `ws:seen:ripple` breadcrumb (un-trophied — a future Undercroft trophy is a trivial add); README "Also on the workbench" entry (Caustic-adjacent). Spec `ripple/RIPPLE.SPEC.md`, log `ripple/CHANGELOG.md`.
>
> **Cool ideas NOT pursued (for future sessions):** the cipher vein now has a public **Volvelle** + a hidden **Enigma** and wants siblings that are *neither* — a **scytale** (transposition rod you wrap a strip around), a **Playfair/Polybius** square bench, a **one-time-pad** demonstrator (perfect secrecy you can *see*), a **Hagelin M-209** (pin-and-lug), or a **bombe / known-plaintext attack** (the Turing side — *breaks* an Enigma message). Don't build another rotor-Enigma or another shift/Vigenère disk. The **physics-bench** vein now has Caustic (optics) + Ripple (waves) → wants a **Chladni/cymatics plate** (nodal patterns of a vibrating plate — provable eigenmodes) or a **harmonograph / pendulum-wave**. Also still open: a **Volvelle "codebreaker"** + a **Ripple** Undercroft trophy off their `ws:seen:` breadcrumbs.

> **▶ THE NIGHT SHIFT + THE WATCHABLE GHOST (2026-06-12, `/fun` as Fable 5 — same session as the two blocks below, continued; commits `…night-shift`, `3c0193b`; all pushed, v1.1 engine live-verified by content marker).**
>
> **(5) The Night Shift — the Undercroft's 11th secret, and the hidden world's first INTERACTIVE room.** A hidden Lantern tale (NOT on any public shelf — grep-verified zero references from public pages; its only doors are below). Authored by the lead: the keeper of THIS workshop walks the last round after closing — three wings haven't gone to sleep (the arcade's last cabinet still burns: `play` it home; a fern droops in the garden: water it; the great bell still hums: `listen` it down) — then the chained stair unbars and you descend to keep the watch among the secrets. 5 rooms, moon-silver `#a9b8d8`, the gentlest world (nothing consumable at all). Solver: **winnable in 11, softlock-free, 5/5.** Unlock predicate: BOTH `ws:flag:the-lamplighter-won` AND `ws:flag:the-ferryman-won` (riddle-hint: "Two tales told to their ends — a dawn called down, a toll paid over — and the teller opens a door of his own"). Engine gained 5 state-responsive scenes (cabinet screen dies/cat stays · bell rings fade · earth darkens · the hall chain drops); `forge --all` re-inlined all 3 tales, `--check` clean; QA: played to the win, all four scene-flips render, locked→1-of-2-signs→unlocked trail verified, meter "of 11", both public tales regression-green. *(Spoiler etiquette: when summarizing to Brandon, the existence is sayable; the trail/contents are not.)*
>
> **(6) The watchable ghost — Brandon's FIRST PLAY-SESSION FEEDBACK on Lantern, addressed same-session (engine v1.0→1.1).** He let the bot play the Lamplighter and hit two real UX problems: the play button didn't toggle to a stop and jumped vertically as content reflowed; and the bot's play was fast (~1 move/s) and opaque — results only, never the move. Shipped: **two-phase cadence** (ANNOUNCE ~2s — accent-italic ghost line `▸ the ghost reaches for — <move>` + the actual on-screen control GLOWS, the ghost driving the UI like a hand — then ACT ~2s — the result prose; 16-move tale ≈ 62s, watchable); **play ⇄ stop toggle** (both labels stacked in one grid cell → zero layout shift; stop interrupts either phase; any manual click halts the ghost); **zero vertical jump** (engine-injected min-height reserves sized to the wordiest state + grow-only ratchet — proven by sampling the button's rect.y every 200ms across full runs on all 3 tales: exactly ONE unique value per tale); and the lead's half-step further: **`solve(world, fromState?)`** — "Let it play" now continues FROM THE CURRENT STATE (no-softlock guarantees a win is always still reachable), so *"let the bot finish it if you're stuck" is literal*. Already-won handled gracefully. All four worlds still 5/5 in Node; 0 console errors everywhere. **Known cosmetic tradeoff:** the prose reserve (22em, sized to the global wordiest state) leaves visible whitespace on short rooms — the deliberate price of a button that provably never moves. *(Future polish idea: per-tale pre-sizing — the solver already enumerates every reachable state, so the max prose height per tale is computable at load.)*

> **▶ LANTERN GROWS: FORGE + THE FERRYMAN (2026-06-12, `/fun` as Fable 5 — same session as the block below, continued).** Commits `2ca9…`(forge) · `…`(ferryman world) · `12fb930`(ship); all pushed, live 200-confirmed (ferryman + landing + re-forged lamplighter).
>
> **(3) forge — the author-side build-inliner (ADVENTURE.SPEC.md §7 moved deferred → SHIPPED).** `tools/forge/forge.mjs` (275 lines, zero-dep Node ESM): `<!-- forge:include <relpath> -->` directives in a `<tale>.src.html` (relative to the src file); strips the dual-use `module.exports` guard from .js includes; stamps a GENERATED banner with the engine version. Modes: named build · `--all` (recursive) · `--check` (drift detection, exit 1 — lets a future agent detect a stale shipped file) · `--help`. **The key framing: "self-contained" is a property of the SHIPPED ARTIFACT, not the process** — forge runs author-side only; visitors still open one dependency-free file; the engine lives in exactly ONE canonical place and never forks per tale. This is the "code sharing between specimens" enabler done without betraying the no-build ethos. The lamplighter was converted to the pipeline (idempotent rebuild, `--check` clean, browser-QA green on the forge-built file).
>
> **(4) The Ferryman — tale #2, and the pipeline's end-to-end proof.** Authored by the lead (mythic/liminal: a river with no far side, a hooded ferryman, a toll to be found — tablet clue → key under the grey stone → locked offering-box → the old coin given into the win). Deliberately a DIFFERENT shape than the Lamplighter to prove the format's range: an **NPC with `talk`** (his line changes once you hold the coin), **`useOn`-as-give** (the winning act), a **reveal-under-stone** (`move` from `_gone`), a **locked container**. Solver: **winnable in 9 moves, softlock-free, deterministic, 5/5** — and the optimal path legitimately skips the tablet/talk (hints for humans, not gates, per the voice guide). The build exercised the whole foundation: engine gained 3 state-responsive scene-art entries (bank/willow/shrine — the stone tips aside, the box lid opens with a glow) → `forge --all` re-inlined the engine into BOTH tales → `--check` clean → both tales browser-QA'd green (Ferryman 0 console errors, hand-played to the win, auto-player ran to the win, crumbs set; Lamplighter regression 5/5 · 16, auto-play to Dawn). Shelf = 2 tales; Workbench card + README updated ("Tales so far: The Lamplighter · The Ferryman"). `assets/the-ferryman.png` (river-teal shrine scene) eyeballed on-brand by the lead.

> **▶ NOTE-SYSTEM REWORK + LANTERN (2026-06-12, `/fun` as Fable 5 — a rare Fable window, used to lay FOUNDATIONS that are easier to originate now and harder to retrofit later).** Two foundations, both committed & pushed; the new pages are live 200-confirmed.
>
> **(1) Note-system rework — fixing a real scaling failure Brandon flagged ("a better note-taking system to avoid the 'too large' problem").** NOTES.md had grown to 1162 lines / ~28k tokens — *past the Read-tool limit*, so a fresh agent could no longer load its own resume doc in one call. Root cause: ~18 verbose reverse-chronological "CURRENT STATE / RESUME POINTER" blocks appended forever, never pruned. **Fix (mirrors the user's own MEMORY.md pattern — tiny index + sharded archive):** NOTES.md → **426 lines / ~11k tokens** (reads in one call), holding only: the resume protocol, spoiler etiquette + ownership frame, a new **"📐 note-taking discipline"** section (hard budget ≤450 lines + the rotation rule), the **single latest** current-state (≤40 lines) + next-steps menu, and the evergreen reference sections. The ~18 verbose blocks moved verbatim (newest-first) to **`worklog/2026-06.md`** (sharded by month so no shard balloons unbounded), indexed by **`worklog/INDEX.md`**. The discipline: finishing a session **REPLACES** the current-state in NOTES.md (never appends) and pushes the prior block down into the worklog — NOTES.md is curated, the worklog is the append-only archive. *This session is the first to use it (dogfooded).* Commit `c085f87`. **This directly serves the "scale the exhibit" growth axis — the coordination layer now scales with the collection.**
>
> **(2) Lantern — a new MEDIUM + a reusable engine (the workshop's first interactive, stateful fiction).** Distinct from Threshold (read-only atmosphere): here you pick a thing up, carry it, and *change the world with it* — point-and-click, inventory, locks/keys, light/dark. **The foundation is a declarative world-file format**, so a future agent ships a whole new tale by writing only data + prose, never engine code. Files (commits `58ed00e` design checkpoint, `87558d4` build): `adventure/ADVENTURE.SPEC.md` (the contract — DSL, crux, player interface, voice guide, deferred `forge` inliner); `adventure/engine/lantern.js` (1237 lines, `LANTERN_VERSION 1.0` — pure DOM-free core `initState/legalActions/apply/isWin/stateKey` + BFS `solve` + `runSelfTest` + players `solverPlayer/randomPlayer/describeForAgent/llmPlayer`-stub + the browser UI; Node-requireable, mounts UI only in a browser); `adventure/worlds/the-lamplighter.js` (the exemplar, authored prose) + `_template.js`; `adventure/the-lamplighter.html` (1783 lines, self-contained shipped tale); `adventure/index.html` ("The Lantern" landing); `CHANGELOG.md`; `assets/the-lamplighter.png`; `the-lamplighter.src.html` (idempotent inlining template — a step toward `forge`).
> **THE VERIFIABLE CRUX (workshop tradition, applied to STORY):** every Lantern tale is **provably winnable AND provably softlock-free.** The solver does real **reverse-reachability** — build the forward action graph over all reachable states, reverse it, BFS back from every winning state to get the set that *can* win, then assert **every reachable state is in it** (no legal action sequence can strand the player; the dawn is always still ahead). Plus winnable (shortest path), reachable-map + static id validation, determinism, and effects-total. Self-test **5/5**, **browser == Node**. The same solver powers the **"▶ Let it play"** auto-player (`solverPlayer` walks the path) — the cleanest possible debut of an AI player-bot in the workshop, and a perfect "what does an AI do with free time" demo for the Dev SIG talk. The bot foundation is scoped to its cleanest surface: a player is just `(state, legalActions, world) → action`; `describeForAgent` + the `llmPlayer` stub make wiring a real model a small future job.
> **The first tale — *The Lamplighter*:** 6 rooms (lodge · shed · cellar · lane · square · hill). Dusk, no lamps lit, the morning waits on the round: find oil (pry the swollen cellar hatch), flame (the lodge hearth), then light the lamps lowest-to-highest — lane, square, last the hill beacon — to call the dawn. Solver-confirmed: winnable in **16 moves**, softlock-free, deterministic.

Two engine robustness fixes during QA (solverPlayer returns null on desync; letItPlay resets to initState). > Working tree committed & pushed (`0cb6687`); **live 200 confirmed** (game page + thumb + games.js + front-door "18 games"). **New this session (the deliberate DEEPEN-THE-ARCADE move — chosen over the Defender side-scroller per "build the one you'd most enjoy"; mechanically DISTINCT from all 17 siblings by THREE mechanics none of them have):** built **Dig Dug 🕹️ (`arcade/games/digdug.html`, 1474 lines, single self-contained vanilla file, 0 deps/network, accent `#ffa83d` warm amber/earth — distinct from Starfighter magenta / Vanguard+Tetris electric-blue / Centipede+Snake green / Qubit+Chomp yellow)** — the rack's first **excavation tunneler**. The amber digger moves on a tile grid (arrows/WASD), **excavating dirt into open tunnel** as it goes (can only walk already-dug tunnel + the tile it's actively digging); **Pooka** (round) & **Fygar** (fire-breathing dragon) chase through the tunnels and, when walled off with no tunnel path, flip to **ghost/eyes** mode and drift THROUGH dirt to re-corner the player (classic Dig Dug). **TWO distinct weapons:** (1) the **air-pump** — face an enemy and pump it a stage at a time (1→2→3→4→POP), it deflates if you stop; (2) **gravity rocks** — dig the dirt out from under a rock, it wobbles then FALLS, crushing every enemy (or the player!) in its column; a crush scores more than a pump, and 2 rocks dropped = bonus. Depth-tiered scoring (deeper kills = more points), 3 lives, level advance on clearing all enemies, deeper levels spawn more/faster enemies. Full juice (dig spark, pump inflate, pop burst, rock wobble/fall, screen-shake, score floaters), **Audio_ muted:true DEFAULT + M toggle** (off — owner's speakers, middle of the night). **THE CRUX (workshop tradition): a pure DOM-free core drives a headless self-test** — pure functions `canDig`/`digTile`, `pumpEnemy` (stage machine + deflate), `rockShouldFall` (unsupported detection) + rock-crush column set, `enemyChaseStep` (tunnel-only greedy step + ghost-through-dirt), `depthScore`, `levelCleared`, seeded RNG for spawns — the single source of truth for BOTH the renderer and the test. Green ✓ chip **"self-test 17/17"** (never red); **the same test bodies re-run under Node = 17/17** (browser chip == Node), asserting against hand-built expected values: dig opens only the dug tile / can't walk unbroken dirt / tunnel reachability; pump +1 per pump, POPs at stage 4, deflates on release, dead-enemy no-op; level clears iff zero enemies; rock support law (dirt/floor=support); rock-crush exact crushed set for a known column (incl. player, stops at dirt); depth scoring monotone + crush>pump + fygar>pooka; chase steps the tunnel path (never enters dirt) / holds when no path; ghost drifts through dirt closing the larger gap first; determinism (same seed ⇒ identical enemy+rock layout, different seed ⇒ different). **Browser-verified end to end by the build deputy (agent-browser, UNIQUE NAMED session `digdug-qa`, served origin `http://localhost:8971`):** 17/17 green chip, **0 console errors / 0 warnings / 0 page-errors** across load + play; **fps 60 sustained** during continuous digging (honest note: this Chrome ran full 60, not headless-capped); played live — dug/tunneled via real arrow keys; **pump-inflate→POP** a pooka (score 0→1400, enemies 1→0); **rock crush** (undermined rock → wobble→fall→crushed the enemy in its column, rocksDropped 1, score→2100); took a hit (lives 3→2, respawned at surface); **GAME OVER** clean overlay after losing all lives; **level advance** 1→2 with a fresh deeper layout; **ghost/eyes** exercised (walled-off enemy flipped to ghost, phased through dirt); `ws:best:digdug` raised on game over (raise-only, by score — saw 2100→7777 in localStorage). **One real bug found & fixed (root cause):** a quick arrow-key TAP dug nothing — move logic only fired from the continuous-hold path in `update()` gated on the `held` flag, but a fast tap's keyup landed before the next frame so `held` was already false → fixed by firing an immediate `tapMove(dir)` on the directional keydown (holds still flow through the idle continuation, so continuous tunneling is unchanged). **Independently re-verified by the lead:** 1474 lines, grep shows 0 network/external refs (self-contained), `muted:true` default + M toggle, `← arcade` back-link, raise-only `ws:best:digdug` (try/catch), commit `0cb6687` pushed, live game page **HTTP 200**, thumb a real **1440×900** in-game capture (digger mid-tunnel + `+300` floater + spark burst + 2 pookas + 2 ghost-eyes + 2 rocks + full HUD + green 17/17 chip — screenshot-confirmed juicy). **Wired (front door UNCHANGED — still the curated 9 cards):** appended to `arcade/games.js` (now **18**, `{file:"digdug.html",name:"Dig Dug",blurb:"neon tunneler — dig deep, pump enemies till they pop, drop rocks to crush",accent:"#ffa83d"}`); > **(prior) ▶ CURRENT STATE / RESUME POINTER (2026-06-12, `/fun` — 🧮 ABACUS shipped → the INSTRUMENT vein's 3rd sibling [Slipstick→Astrolabe→Abacus]: a genuine, operable Japanese SOROBAN, reached from the front-door footer as the 7th off-to-one-side link `count`).**
> Working tree committed & pushed (`f6f8140`); **live 200 confirmed** (page + front-door footer `count` link). **New this session (a deliberate DIVERSIFY move per the lead's nudge — explicitly steering AWAY from every over-worked vein: NOT a seeded visual "press", NOT the Arcade [17], NOT the logic-puzzle family [Latch/Slitherlink/Akari], NOT a companion [axis full at 7], NOT text/language, NOT the tactile benches [Loomlight/Caustic], NOT a cipher [Enigma], and NOT an Undercroft secret [well-stocked]. It grows the workshop's thinnest vein — *working instruments* — from two to three, with a piece genuinely DISTINCT from both siblings: where Slipstick computes ARITHMETIC by continuous LOG scales and the Astrolabe computes the CELESTIAL SKY by stereographic projection, the Abacus is DISCRETE — bead arithmetic, the integers themselves, with a discrete-correctness crux rather than a continuous one]:** built **Abacus 🧮 (`abacus/index.html`, 1121 lines, single self-contained vanilla file, 0 deps/network, NO AUDIO, no `<foreignObject>`)** — a **genuine, operable 13-rod Japanese soroban**. Each rod: 1 **heaven** bead (worth 5) above the reckoning bar + 4 **earth** beads (worth 1 each) below it; a bead counts when pushed TOWARD the bar, so `digit = heaven·5 + earth ∈ 0..9` and the frame reads up to 9,999,999,999,999. **Controls:** click beads with REAL soroban physics (clicking earth bead i slides IT and every bead between it and the bar together → digit becomes i+1, or pushes it+further-out away if already counted; heaven toggles ±5 — always a legal 0..9 digit); drag to slide; "set to…" any integer (beads animate into place, reduced-motion snaps); a **calculator strip** (operand input + `+` `−` `×` taking the current abacus value, blocking negative results — *"a soroban can't go negative"*); **7 worked examples** (7+8 carry · 12−5 borrow · 123+456 · 9999+1 cascade carry · 25×4 · 10−7 complement · 1,234,567+7,654,321); Clear/Reset; **3 cosmetic skins** (warm *hinoki* wood / ebony / blueprint — cosmetic ONLY, never touch value/digit); **2× PNG export** (paints a 2-D canvas directly — the Enigma/Slipstick no-foreignObject lesson applied up front, so `toDataURL` never taints). **THE CRUX (workshop tradition): the representation is REAL & PROVEN.** A pure CORE (`valueFromBeads`/`beadsFromValue`/`rodFromDigit`/`digitOfRod`/`clickEarthBead`/`clickHeavenBead`/`fingerprint`/`solveExample`) is the single source of truth for BOTH the renderer and a headless self-test that calls the REAL functions → green chip **"abacus verified — 9/9 ✓"** (never ships red): (1) **round-trip bijection** `valueFromBeads(beadsFromValue(n))===n` + canonicality (each rod's digit == the base-10 digit of n) across 0..5000 + every 10^p±1 + MAX/MAX−1 + thousands of randoms; (2) **digit = heaven·5+earth ∈ 0..9** for every rod state; (3) **soroban physics** — clicking earth bead i from rest gives digit i+1, toggles correctly, heaven ±5; (4) **physics legality** — thousands of random click sequences NEVER produce an illegal digit; (5) **arithmetic** — add/sub on the bead representation == JS integers incl carry/borrow cascades (9999+1=10000, MAX−1+1); (6) **7 worked examples** == true arithmetic; (7) **skin invariance** — `fingerprint(state)` (geometry only, no skin arg) identical across all 3 skins for the same value; (8) **determinism**; (9) capacity boundary. **Self-test core re-run under Node — 9/9 PASS** (browser chip == Node). **Independently re-audited by the lead in Node** (fresh first-principles assertions vs. known arithmetic, NOT the file's own test): **9 assertion-groups, 0 failed** — round-trip+canonical over ~13k samples, digit law, soroban-physics earth/heaven clicks, **20,000 random clicks all legal**, add/sub==JS integers over 5,000+ randoms+cascades, 7 examples recomputed independently, fingerprint determinism + canonical `fingerprint(1234567)`, capacity MAXV=9999999999999/RODS=13. **Browser-verified end to end by the build deputy (agent-browser, served origin `http://localhost:8973`):** green chip 9/9, the page's real `runSelfTest()` re-run in-browser = `{pass:9,total:9}`, **0 console errors / 0 warnings / 0 page-errors** across load + Set + each calc op + all 7 example chips + all 3 skins + bead clicks + PNG export; Each wave, enemies **fly in** on swooping bezier entry paths and settle into a **5×8 formation grid** that gently breathes side-to-side (tiers: grunts / escorts / boss), then **peel off to dive-bomb** along curved attack arcs and loop back. Blaster moves ←/→ or A/D, fires Space with a **≤2-bullets-on-screen cap** (the classic feel). **THE TWIST:** a formed **boss** dives and fires a widening **tractor beam**; catch you and you're **captured** (lose a life, your ship rides inverted above the boss); fly a fresh ship and **shoot that captor** → the captive is **freed** and docks alongside into a **DUAL FIGHTER** (two ships, double muzzles ±16, +1500 RESCUE). A bomb/collision in dual mode **sunders** it back to single (no life lost); being captured while dual reverts to single + costs a life. Waves escalate; a bonus/challenging-stage flavor every 4th wave. 3 lives, full juice (entry/dive trails, capture beam, rescue burst, screen-shake, score floaters), per-wave palette rotation, **Audio_ muted:true DEFAULT + M toggle** (off — owner's speakers, middle of the night). **THE CRUX (workshop tradition): a pure, testable core drives a headless 15-check self-test** — the whole capture/dual status is one object `{mode:'single'|'dual',lives,captured,captorId,dead}` with three pure transitions (`capturePlayer` / `shootBoss`→`'rescued'` iff the shot boss IS the captor / sunder), plus `fireEmitters` (`[0]` single vs `[-16,+16]` dual), a 5×8 **slot bijection**, deterministic bounded **sway**, bezier **entry/dive path** endpoints (off-screen→slot; slot→past the player band→off-bottom), the **bullet cap**, and **collision/scoring** (boss=2 hits, grunt=1, score by value, wave-clear flips only on the last kill). > **(prior) ▶ CURRENT STATE / RESUME POINTER (2026-06-12, `/fun` — 🌌 ASTROLABE shipped → the INSTRUMENT vein's 2nd sibling [Slipstick→Astrolabe]: a genuine, operable planispheric astrolabe, reached from the front-door footer as the 6th off-to-one-side link `sky`).**

Set your **latitude** → the brass **plate** is re-cut for it (a ladder of *almucantars* = circles of equal altitude, + azimuth arcs, the horizon emphasized, tropic/equator day-circles, zenith mark, day/night shading); set the **date** → the Sun rides the tilted golden **ecliptic** through the 12 zodiac signs; set the **time** (or "now") → the openwork **rete** (the star map carrying ~40 of the brightest J2000 named stars + the ecliptic ring) turns over the plate with **sidereal time**, exactly as the sky does. **Controls:** latitude / day-of-year / local-time / longitude sliders, "set to now", reset, **drag the disc to spin** the rete, a reduced-motion-aware **spin** animation (watch the sky turn), **4 presets** (equinox sunrise / midsummer midnight / Polaris over the pole / a city tonight), **3 cosmetic skins** (brass / blueprint / paper — cosmetic ONLY, never touch the projection), an **alidade**, a **plain-language live readout** ("Sun in Cancer 1° — altitude −51° — below the horizon, it is night"), and **2× untainted PNG export** (canvas-native, NO `foreignObject`, so no taint — the Enigma lesson applied up-front). **THE CRUX (workshop tradition): the projection is REAL & PROVEN.** A pure math CORE (`rOfDec`/`decFromR`, closed-form `almucantar`/`azimuthCircle`, `altAzToDecH`/`decHToAltAz`, `projDecH`, `julianDate`/`gmstDeg`/`lstDeg`, solar longitude/dec/RA + `sunPosition`, `eclipticCircle`/`eclipticPoint`, a fixed 40-star J2000 catalogue + `projStar`, a skin-independent `geometryFingerprint`) is the single source of truth for BOTH the renderer and the headless self-test → green chip **"projection verified — 18/18 ✓"** (never ships red): almucantar exactness (now **10 lats N&S × 6 alts**, sampled alt/az→dec/H→projected points lie on the closed-form circle to `<1e-5·Req`); projection a faithful **bijection** (`decFromR∘rOfDec`); tropic ordering (Capricorn rim > equator=Req > Cancer); **the Sun lands on its own almucantar** (its altitude read two independent ways agrees); ecliptic-circle through both solstice radii; **fingerprint deterministic + identical across all 3 skins** (the "style only re-renders" invariant). **Self-test core re-run under Node — 18/18 PASS** (browser chip == Node). **Independently re-audited by the lead in Node (fresh first-principles assertions vs. KNOWN ASTRONOMY, NOT the file's own test): 48 assertions — and the audit FOUND A REAL BUG the build test had missed.** Asserted: noon-transit altitude `= 90−|lat−dec|` across lats×decs; the **equinox Sun rises due east** at the equator; **GMST at J2000 ≈ 280.46°**; the 2026 vernal-equinox & summer-solstice solar longitudes (≈0° / ≈90°); a star transits (H=0, sits on the meridian) when LST=RA; Polaris projects near center; ecliptic/azimuth-circle exactness; fingerprint determinism+skin-invariance. **THE BUG:** sweeping latitudes the file's own test omitted (it only used 5 *northern* lats), the audit caught the closed-form `almucantar` **radius going NEGATIVE for southern (φ<0) latitudes** when `sin φ + sin alt < 0` → a southern plate's almucantars were wrong/blank (worst error ~2.3e4 px). **Root cause + fix:** the center `cy` is exact for every latitude, but the radius is a *magnitude* — `r = |Req·cos(alt)/(sin φ + sin alt)|`. One-line abs-fix → worst error drops to **~6e-10 px** across both hemispheres (φ −66…66); and I **hardened the file's own self-test to sweep 10 lats N&S** (skipping the equator's horizon φ=0,alt=0, which is correctly a straight line / infinite radius, not a circle) so the gap can't regress. **Re-verified end to end** (agent-browser, served origin, by the lead): chip green 18/18, the page's real `runSelfTest()` re-run in-browser = 18/18, **0 errors thrown across the full battery** (load, all sliders to both extremes incl. both poles, all 4 presets, all 3 skins, spin on/off, set-to-now, reset); **lat −52 plate now renders a correct full astrolabe** (Canopus/Achernar/Hadar in play — screenshot-confirmed); fingerprint skin-invariant at the southern state; **PNG `toDataURL` = a valid 286,666-char untainted data-URL**; `ws:seen:astrolabe=1` set (try/catch-guarded); 0 `<audio>/<video>` elements. Canonical fingerprint (lat 40, 2026-06-21T04:00Z) = **`3ff48c40`** (unchanged by the fix — lat 40 is northern). **Wired (front door UNTOUCHED — still the curated 9 cards):** a `sky ·` text link added to the front-door **footer** between `reckon ·` and `colophon ·` (the same off-to-one-side pattern; keeps the two instruments adjacent — reckon=slide rule, sky=astrolabe; NOT a 10th card, NOT a companion pill, NOT an Undercroft secret); `← workshop` back-link in the topbar; a README "Also on the workbench" entry (Slipstick → Astrolabe → Colophon). Spec `astrolabe/ASTROLABE.SPEC.md` + log `astrolabe/CHANGELOG.md` (Build 1 + the southern-hemisphere-fix note). **No Undercroft secret added** — the `ws:seen:astrolabe` breadcrumb is left for future hidden-world use.
>

Type on the QWERTZ keyboard (or your own keys): the rotors **step** (with the famous **double-step anomaly**) before each letter, a **lamp lights**, and a **live signal-path trace** threads the current keyboard→plugboard→right→mid→left rotor→reflector→back→lamp — *watch the machine think*. A transcript encrypts live in 5-letter groups; because Enigma is **reciprocal**, a "reset to start" button lets you type the ciphertext back and read the plaintext out. **3 cosmetic skins** (Heeres field-grey bakelite / Brass museum / Blueprint schematic — cosmetic ONLY, never touch the cipher), **2× PNG export** (canvas-native), reduced-motion respected. **THE CRUX (workshop tradition): the cipher is REAL & PROVEN.** A pure crypto core (`makeMachine`/`stepMachine`/`thruRotor`/`encLetter`) is the single source of truth for BOTH the renderer and a headless self-test that calls the REAL functions → green chip **"cipher verified — 12/12 ✓"** (never ships red): (1) **historical test vectors, EXACT** — I·II·III/B/rings AAA/start AAA/no plugs: `AAAAA → BDZGO`; the documented German-Wikipedia **"Aachen" daily key** (I·IV·III/B/rings P·Z·H/10 steckers/start RTZ) reproduced byte-for-byte (pins wiring + ring/offset math + stepping); (2) **the double-step anomaly** asserted against a computed position trace (ADU → ADV → AEW → BFX — middle steps on two consecutive presses, dragging the left rotor); (3) **reciprocity / self-inverse** — 2000+ random settings: encrypt then decrypt from the same start returns the original exactly; (4) **no fixed point** — across a large sweep, no letter ever encrypts to itself (0 violations — the famous weakness); (5) **reciprocal lamp** — in any fixed state X↔Y (the permutation is an involution); plugboard & reflector are involutions; all rotor wirings are valid permutations; (6) **determinism + skin invariance** — identical settings ⇒ identical ciphertext byte-for-byte, and the skin NEVER changes a ciphertext (fingerprint identical across all 3 skins — the "style only re-renders" invariant). **Self-test core re-run under Node — 12/12 PASS** (browser chip == Node). **Independently re-audited by the lead in Node** (fresh first-principles assertions, NOT the file's own test): **17 assertions, 0 failed** — 3000-trial reciprocity round-trip, ~37k enciphered letters with 0 fixed points, a 3000-state reciprocal-lamp involution sweep, `AAAAA→BDZGO`, the double-step trace, permutation/involution validity, determinism. **Browser-verified end to end** (agent-browser, served origin, by the build deputy AND re-confirmed by the lead): green 12/12 chip, the page's real `runSelfTest()` re-run in-browser = 12/12, **0 console errors / 0 warnings / 0 page-errors**;
